`\"PORTAL_1A/1B/2A/2B\"` | 双子传送门 | `80G/对` | `2 对` | **免疫** | 必须成对；每回合至多传送 2 次。 |\n74: \n75: ---\n76: \n77: ## 三、 v2.2 物理引擎与新机关精确判定语义\n78: \n79: ### 1. `HIGH_WALL` 与实时门态\n80: - **`is_plates_active()`（纯实时派生，永不陈旧）**：当且仅当地图存在至少 1 个 `PLATE`，且所有 `PLATE` 上均有 `occupant == \"BOX\"` 时返回 `True`。无论是在推箱子子步（Step 3 变更 `occupant`）、还是阶段 C/D 爆炸炸毁或填坑消耗 `BOX` 后，`plates_active` 立刻重新计算生效。\n81: - **`HIGH_WALL` 集合**：`W`, `T_W`, 全部 8 种 `LASER_* / LASER2_*` 炮座，关闭状态的 `R_GATE` / `B_GATE`，以及当 `not plates_active` 时的 `P_GATE`。\n82: - **闭门滞留规则**：若实体停留在开着的门内时开关翻转或压板松开导致关门，实体不受挤压伤害：勇者仍可在原地停留或主动走向外侧合法格离开；**被困在关闭门格内的 `BOX` 保留占用，但从门外推动它需要先开门（因外侧进门方向受高墙阻挡）**。\n83: \n84: ### 2. `ONEWAY_U/D/L/R` 全场景统一裁决\n85: 记 `blocks_oneway(x, y, dir)` 为真，当且仅当 `terrain[y][x] == \"ONEWAY_<d>\"` 且本次进入方向向量 `dir != DIR_VECTORS[d]`：\n86: 1. **普通 `MOVE_*` 与推箱**：前置先验 `(x1, y1)`（若该格上停着一个 `BOX`，逆向推动会直接整次拒绝，**箱子绝不移动且脚下 `CRACK` 不坍塌**！）；若推箱子，`BOX` 目标格 `p2` 验 `dir`，箱链完成后勇者跟进 `p1` 再验 `dir`。\n87: 2. **主动 `JUMP_*`**：中间飞越格 `p1` **与** 落点格 `p2` 若均不阻挡（非高墙且均不 `blocks_oneway(..., jump_dir)`）方可执行（受阻失败仍扣 1 次跳跃，但脚下 `CRACK` 不坍塌）。\n88: 3. **冰面与折跃斜坡**：`ICE` 按当前 `dir` 检查下一格；**`MIRROR_*` 先折转得到 `new_dir`，再用 `new_dir` 检查目标格**。\n89: 4. **弹簧 `SPRING_*`**：沿 `sdir` 逐格检测 $k=1,2,3$；若在第 $k$ 格遇逆向 `ONEWAY_*` 则视作阻挡停于 `k-1`（$k=1$ 时留在弹簧原地）。\n90: 5. **传送门 `PORTAL_*`**：从 A 传送到 B 格本身不验向量；到达配对端 B 后保持原方向 `dir` 尝试向前迈出 1 格时，若前方格为逆向 `ONEWAY_*` 则被拦住并停稳在配对端 B 上。\n91: 6. **飞石 `THROW_*`**：**空中 6 格飞行自由穿过所有方向的 `ONEWAY_*`**（正常消耗射程）；若第 6 格落入 `ICE`/`MIRROR_*` 转入地面滑行，滑行撞到 `ONEWAY_*` 时按普通非冰地面立即停稳消失。\n92: 7. **激光**：`LASER_*` 与 `LASER2_*` 光束自由穿过所有方向的 `ONEWAY_*`（但仍被站在 `ONEWAY_*` 上的 `BOX` 遮挡）。\n93: \n94: ### 3. 双频激光（阶段 D & `laser_warnings`）\n95: - 仅当 **`turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0`** 时进入阶段 D：\n96:   - `turn % 3 == 0`：所有存活红炮 `LASER_*`（`period=3`）开火；\n97:   - `turn % 4 == 0`：所有存活蓝炮 `LASER2_*`（`period=4`）开火；\n98:   - **`turn % 12 == 0`**：红炮与蓝炮**基于阶段 C 结束后的同一快照同步齐射**，叠加命中不同炮座分别计 `50` 伤（被 2 门以上命中即 `-100 HP` 瞬间暴毙！），同时击中同一 `TNT` 仅在该批爆炸闭包引爆一次，不撤销本拍已发射激光。\n99: - **`state[\"laser_warnings\"]`**：当回合会发射激光的回合返回当前阶段 A 前快照下所有可见预警射线格：`{\"x\": bx, \"y\": by, \"dir\": \"U/D/L/R\", \"period\": 3|4, \"cannon\": [lx, ly]}`（按 `(y, x, dir, period, cannon)` 排序）。\n100: \n101: ---\n102: \n103: ## 四、 计分公式与接口 Schema\n104: \n105: - **勇者分 `Score_Hero`（同 v2.1）**：\n106:   - 通关：$600 + 1.5 \\times HP_{\\text{left}} + 150 \\times \\frac{240 - T}{239} + 100 \\times g$\n107:   - 失败/超时/DQ：$200 \\times k + 200 \\times r$\n108: - **魔王分 `Score_Demon`**：\n109:   - **实杀 `DEAD`**：$500 + 50 \\times (3 - P) + D + 250 \\times g$\n110:   - **困死 `TIMEOUT` 或对方接口弃权 `DQ`**：$320 + 50 \\times (3 - P) + D + 125 \\times g$\n111:   - **金库失守 `CLEARED`（v2.2 零和微调）**：$1.7 \\times D + 150 \\times \\frac{T - 1}{239} \\in [0, 320.0]$\n112: - **`decide_action(state, memory)` 输入新增字段**：\n113:   - `state[\"mode\"]` 恒为 `\"FULL\"`；\n114:   - `state[\"plates_active\"]`：`bool`，当前全场压板门是否全部开启；\n115:   - `state[\"laser_warnings\"]`：每项包含 `\"x\", \"y\", \"dir\", \"period\", \"cannon\"`。"
```json
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

==============================================================================\n191: \n192: def _in_bounds(x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n193:     return 0 <= x < GRID_WIDTH and 0 <= y < GRID_HEIGHT\n194: \n195: \n196: def _is_high_wall_tile(t: str, gates_flipped: bool) -> bool:\n197:     if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n198:         return True\n199:     if not gates_flipped and t == \"R_GATE\":\n200:         return True\n201:     if gates_flipped and t == \"B_GATE\":\n202:         return True\n203:     return False\n204: \n205: \n206: def _init_memory(state: dict, memory: dict) -> None:\n207:     if memory[\"tried_throws\"] = set()\n236:     memory[\"tried_portals\"] = set()\n237:     memory[\"portal_dest\"] = {}\n238:     memory[\"last_pos\"] = None\n239:     memory[\"last_action\"] = None\n240:     memory[\"switches_flipped\"] = 0\n241:     memory[\"last_gates_flipped\"] = bool(state.get(\"gates_flipped\", False))\n242: \n243: \n244: def _update_memory(state: dict, memory: dict) -> None:\n245:     _init_memory(state, memory)\n246:     terrain = memory[\"terrain\"]\n247:     occupant = memory[\"occupant\"]\n248:     vis = state[\"visible_mask\"]\n249:     obs_t = state[\"hero\"][\"y\"]\n255:     gates_flipped = bool(state.get(\"gates_flipped\", False))\n256: \n257:     if gates_flipped != memory[\"last_gates_flipped\"]:\n258:         memory[\"switches_flipped\"] += 1\n259:         memory[\"last_gates_flipped\"] = gates_flipped\n260: \n261:     last_pos = memory[\"last_pos\"]\n262:     last_act = memory[\"last_action\"]\n263:     if last_pos is not None and last_act is not None:\n264:         lx, ly = last_pos\n265:         if abs(hx - lx) + abs(hy - ly) > 4:\n266:             memory[\"portal_dest\"][(lx, ly, last_act)] = (hx, hy)\n267: \n268:     for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n269:         row_vis = vis[y]\n270:         for x in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n271:             if row_vis[x]:\n272:                 t = obs_t[y][x]\n273:                 terrain[y][x] = t\n274:                 occupant[y][x] = obs_o[y][x]\n275:                 memory[\"seen_ever\"].add((x, y))\n276:                 if obs_c[y][x]:\n277:                     memory[\"clue_cells\"].add((x, y))\n278:                 else:\n279:                     memory[\"clue_cells\"].discard((x, y))\n280: \n281:     keys = state[\"hero\"][\"keys\"]\n282:     targets = state[\"targets\"]\n283:     if not keys[0]:\n284:         k1x, k1y = targets[\"K1\"]\n285:         if terrain[k1y][k1x] == \"UNKNOWN\":\n286:             terrain[k1y][k1x] = \"K1\"\n287:     if not keys[1]:\n288:         k2x, k2y = targets[\"K2\"]\n289:         if terrain[k2y][k2x] == \"UNKNOWN\":\n290:             terrain[k2y][k2x] = \"K2\"\n291:     vx, vy = targets[\"V\"]\n292:     if []):\n298:             wx, wy, wdir = w[\"x\"], w[\"y\"], w[\"dir\"]\n299:             warn_coords.add((wx, wy))\n300:             memory[\"confirmed_laser_cells\"].add((wx, wy))\n301:             memory[\"laser_beams_by_dir\"].add((wx, wy, wdir))\n302:             dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[wdir]\n303:             for step_i in range(1, 10):\n304:                 nx, ny = wx + step_i * dx, wy + y))\n315: \n316:     memory[\"visit_counts\"][(hx, hy)] += 1\n317:     memory[\"pos_history\"].append((hx, hy))\n318: \n319: \n320: def _classify_chests(memory: dict) -> typing.Tuple[set, set]:\n321: for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n328:         for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n329:             if terrain[y][x] == \"CHEST\":\n330:                 has_clue = False\n331:                 all_seen = True\n332:                 for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n333:                     nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n334:                     if (nx, ny) in clues:\n335: y))\n340:                 elif all_seen:\n341:                     confirmed_heals.add((x, y))\n342:                 else:\n343:                     suspected_mimics.add((x, y))\n344: \n345:     return suspected_mimics, confirmed_heals\n346: \n347: \n348: def _get_laser_hazard_cells(memory: dict, gates_flipped: bool) -> set:\n349:     terrain = memory[\"terrain\"]\n350:     occupant = memory[\"occupant\"]\n351:     hazards = set(memory[\"confirmed_laser_cells\"])\n352: \n353:     for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n354:         for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n355:             t = terrain[y][x]\n356:             if t.startswith(\"LASER_\"):\n357:                 ldir = t[-1]\n358:                 dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[ldir]\n359:                 for dist in range(1, terrain[ry][rx]\n364:                     if rt != \"UNKNOWN\" and _is_high_wall_tile(rt, gates_flipped):\n365:                         break\n366:                     if occupant[ry][rx] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n367:                         break\n368:                     hazards.add((rx, ry))\n369: \n370:     return hazards\n371: \n372: \n373: def _has_unverified_laser_los(memory: dict, x: int, y: t_copy[y1][x1]\n457:         if suppress_portal_once:\n458:             suppress_portal_once = False\n459:             if portal_count >= 2:\n460:                 break\n461:             dx, dy = ## 2026-11-20T01:03:04.377000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=147 message=msg_1f48c1833001bniEj8vF3f5RVH

**Unveiling The Rules**

I've just had a major breakthrough! The rules are crystallizing with absolute clarity. The examples of LASER exposure with glm versus argon at T6 and deepseek versus argon at T75 are now providing sharp insights. The precise effects on position and hit points are revealing the underlying mechanics of this process with impressive precision. It's almost fully solved!


**Refining The Mechanics**

I've made a crucial observation. Analyzing the `hero` versus `qwen` example at T69 and now I see it clearly. A laser cannon deals a maximum of -50 HP to the hero per turn. The fact is, even if the hero's position is exposed to multiple beams from the same cannon, or the same beam hits multiple cells on a turn, the damage is capped at -50 HP per cannon. The key seems to be that a cannon inflicts this single instance of damage if `beam_cells` and `exposed` are not empty on that turn.


**Clarifying The Triggers**

I've just realized something critical about laser beam exposure! If you're standing on a beam at the *end* of turn `3k-1`, you're guaranteed damage on turn `3k`, even if you move! It's because the cell is already marked as 'exposed'. The only exceptions are if the cannon is destroyed or blocked *before* that phase! This impacts both our solver and dungeon design tremendously. And I'm now looking at bomb detonation timing.


**Pinpointing The Bomb Timing**

I've finally cracked the bomb detonation sequence. The crucial observation is that a bomb's timer is decremented at the *end* of each turn. This means a bomb placed at the beginning of turn *t* will detonate during phase C of turn *t+2*. I've verified this with the example scenarios, including the adjacency rule. The code confirms this with the timer check near the return statement on line 935.


**Confirming Explosion Dynamics**

I've just solidified the bomb explosion mechanics. The bomb damage check uses the hero's final position, not the 'exposed' cells. Examining T55 and T68 confirms that the explosion on turn t+2 only considers the hero's final location post-movement, at Phase C. This significantly simplifies the damage calculation and has implications for dungeon design and solver development. My code will need to reflect the precise timing of phase C.

v2.2 physics model.\"\"\"\n\n    def build_from_state(self, state):\n        obs_t = state[\"terrain\"]\n        obs_o = state[\"occupant\"]\n        clues = state[\"clues\"]\n        mimics, heals = _classify_chests_exact(obs_t, clues)\n\n        self.base = [row[:] for row in obs_t]\n        for mx, my in mimics:\n            self.base[my][mx] = \"MIMIC\"\n        for hx, hy in heals:\n            self.base[hy][hx] = \"HEAL\"\n\n        self.plates = []\n        self.pgates = []\n        self.switches = []\n        self.portals = {}\n        self.lasers = []  # list of (x, y, period, dir, destr_bit)\n        self.crack_bit = {}\n        self.pit_bit = {}\n        self.destr_bit = {}\n        self.heals = []\n        self.mimics = []\n        self.has_ice_or_spring = False\n\n        c_cnt = 0\n        p_cnt = 0\n        d_cnt = 0\n        init_boxes = []\n        init_tnts = []\n\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n                t = self.base[y][x]\n                occ = obs_o[y][x]\n                if occ == \"BOX\":\n                    init_boxes.append((x, y))\n                elif occ == \"TNT\":\n                    init_tnts.append((x, y))\n\n                if t == \"PLATE\":\n                    self.plates.append((x, y))\n                elif t == \"P_GATE\":\n                    self.pgates.append((x, y))\n                elif t == \"SWITCH\":\n                    self.switches.append((x, y))\n                elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    self.portals[t] = (x, y)\n                elif t == \"CRACK\":\n                    self.crack_bit[(x, y)] = 1 << c_cnt\n                    c_cnt += 1\n                    self.destr_bit[(x, y)] = 1 << d_cnt\n                    d_cnt += 1\n                elif t == \"PIT\":\n                    self.pit_bit[(x, y)] = 1 << p_cnt\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                elif t == \"HEAL\":\n                    self.heals.append((x, y))\n                elif t == \"MIMIC\":\n                    self.mimics.append((x, y))\n\n                if t in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY or t in (\"K1\", \"K2\"):\n                    if (x, y) not in self.destr_bit:\n                        self.destr_bit[(x, y)] = 1 << d_cnt\n                        d_cnt += 1\n\n                if t in LASER1_TILES:\n                    self.lasers.append((x, y, 3, t[-1], self.destr_bit[(x, y)]))\n                elif t in LASER2_TILES:\n                    self.lasers.append((x, y, 4, t[-1], self.destr_bit[(x, y)]))\n                if t == \"ICE\" or t in MIRROR_MAP or t in SPRING_TILES or t in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    self.has_ice_or_spring = True\n\n        self.plates_tuple = tuple(self.plates)\n        self.plates_set = set(self.plates)\n        self.has_lasers = len(self.lasers) > 0\n        self.targets = state[\"targets\"]\n        self.k1_pos = tuple(self.targets[\"K1\"])\n        self.k2_pos = tuple(self.targets[\"K2\"])\n        self.v_pos = tuple(self.targets[\"V\"])\n\n        self.laser_cache = {}\n        self._precompute_static_analysis()\n\n        hero = state[\"hero\"]\n        kmask = (1 if hero[\"keys\"][0] else 0) | (2 if hero[\"keys\"][1] else 0)\n        init_destr = 0\n        if hero[\"keys\"][0] and self.k1_pos in self.destr_bit:\n            init_destr |= self.destr_bit[self.k1_pos]\n        if hero[\"keys\"][1] and self.k2_pos in self.destr_bit:\n            init_destr |= self.destr_bit[self.k2_pos]\n        init_bombs = tuple((b[\"x\"], b[\"y\"], b[\"timer\"]) for b in state.get(\"bombs\", []))\n\n        init_state = (\n            hero[\"x\"], hero[\"y\"], hero[\"hp\"], kmask,\n            bool(state.get(\"gates_flipped\", False)),\n            hero[\"jumps\"], hero[\"throws\"], hero[\"bombs\"],\n            tuple(sorted(init_boxes)),\n            tuple(sorted(init_tnts)),\n            0, 0, init_destr,\n            init_bombs,\n            state[\"turn\"],\n        )\n        return init_state\n\n    def _precompute_static_analysis(self):\n        # 1. Precompute static distances from K1, K2, V (ignoring gates/boxes/W as lower bound, blocking T_W)\n        def bfs_dist(target_xy):\n            dist = [[999] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n            tx, ty = target_xy\n            dist[ty][tx] = 0\n            q = collections.deque([(tx, ty)])\n            while q:\n                x, y = q.popleft()\n                nd = dist[y][x] + 1\n                for d, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                    px, py = x - dx, y - dy  # reverse step\n                    if _in_bounds(px, py) and dist[py][px] > nd:\n                        t = self.base[py][px]\n                        if t == \"T_W\":\n                            continue\n                        # Reverse oneway check: moving d from (px,py) to (x,y)\n                        cur_t = self.base[y][x]\n                        if cur_t in ONEWAY_TILES and cur_t[-1] != d:\n                            continue\n                        dist[py][px] = nd\n                        q.append((px, py))\n            return dist\n\n        self.dist_k1 = bfs_dist(self.k1_pos)\n        self.dist_k2 = bfs_dist(self.k2_pos)\n        self.dist_v = bfs_dist(self.v_pos)\n        self.dist_plates = [bfs_dist(p) for p in self.plates]\n\n        # 2. Precompute static box-dead corner squares (indestructible 2-wall corners, non-plate, non-switch, non-mech-adj)\n        self.dead_box_cells = set()\n        if self.plates:\n            mech_adj = set()\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    t = self.base[y][x]\n                    if t == \"ICE\" or t in MIRROR_MAP or t in SPRING_TILES or t in PORTAL_PAIRS or t == \"PLATE\" or t == \"SWITCH\":\n                        for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                            for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                                mech_adj.add((x + dx, y + dy))\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    if (x, y) in mech_adj or (x, y) in self.plates_set:\n                        continue\n                    t = self.base[y][x]\n                    if t in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\", \"PLATE\", \"SWITCH\"):\n                        continue\n                    def hard_block(nx, ny, push_dir):\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            return True\n                        nt = self.base[ny][nx]\n                        if nt == \"T_W\" or nt in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                            return True\n                        if nt in ONEWAY_TILES and nt[-1] != push_dir:\n                            return True\n                        return False\n                    up_b = hard_block(x, y - 1, \"U\")\n                    dn_b = hard_block(x, y + 1, \"D\")\n                    lt_b = hard_block(x - 1, y, \"L\")\n                    rt_b = hard_block(x + 1, y, \"R\")\n                    if (up_b or dn_b) and (lt_b or rt_b):\n                        self.dead_box_cells.add((x, y))\n\n        # 3. Precompute candidate useful THROW spots (cells from which throwing in dir d can hit SWITCH/TNT/MIMIC/MIRROR/PORTAL)\n        self.throw_candidates = collections.defaultdict(list)\n        if self.switches or self.mimics or any(self.base[y][x] in PORTAL_PAIRS or self.base[y][x] in MIRROR_MAP for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT) for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)):\n            pass\n        # Fast static ray check ignoring closed gates (we verify dynamically in state!)\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                if self.base[y][x] in (\"T_W\", \"W\", \"PIT\") or self.base[y][x] in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                    continue\n                for d in DIR_LIST:\n                    if self._static_throw_interesting(x, y, d):\n                        self.throw_candidates[(x, y)].append(d)\n\n        # 4. Precompute candidate useful BOMB spots (cells whose 3x3 contains a destructible obstacle)\n        self.bomb_candidates = set()\n        high_val_destr = (\n            LASER1_TILES | LASER2_TILES | {\"R_GATE\", \"B_GATE\", \"MIMIC\", \"SPRING_U\", \"SPRING_D\", \"SPRING_L\", \"SPRING_R\"}\n        )\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                if self.base[y][x] in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\") or self.base[y][x] in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                    continue\n                has_destr = False\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n                        if _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            nt = self.base[ny][nx]\n                            if nt in high_val_destr or nt == \"W\" or nt == \"ICE\" or nt in MIRROR_MAP:\n                                has_destr = True\n                                break\n                    if has_destr:\n                        break\n                if has_destr:\n                    self.bomb_candidates.add((x, y))\n\n    def _static_throw_interesting(self, sx, sy, d):\n        cur_dir = d\n        x, y = sx, sy\n        rng = 6\n        portals_used = 0\n        while rng > 0:\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n            x, y = x + dx, y + dy\n            rng -= 1\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                return False\n            t = self.base[y][x]\n            if t in (\"T_W\", \"W\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                return False\n            if t in (\"SWITCH\", \"MIMIC\"):\n                return True\n            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[t][cur_dir]\n            elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and portals_used < 2:\n                pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                if pp:\n                    x, y = pp\n                    portals_used += 1\n                    if portals_used == 2:\n                        return False\n        # Also check TNT later dynamically; here check if 6th cell is ICE/MIRROR leading to SWITCH/MIMIC\n        if rng == 0:\n            t6 = self.base[y][x]\n            if t6 == \"ICE\" or t6 in MIRROR_MAP:\n                for _ in range(32):\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    x, y = x + dx, y + dy\n                    if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                        return False\n                    t = self.base[y][x]\n                    if t in (\"T_W\", \"W\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                        return False\n                    if t in (\"SWITCH\", \"MIMIC\"):\n                        return True\n                    if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[t][cur_dir]\n                    elif t == \"ICE\":\n                        continue\n                    elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and portals_used < 2:\n                        pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                        if pp:\n                            x, y = pp\n                            portals_used += 1\n                            if portals_used == 2:\n                                return False\n                        else:\n                            return False\n                    else:\n                        return False\n        return False\n\n    def tile_at(self, x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        t = self.base[y][x]\n        if t == \"CRACK\":\n            if destr_m & self.destr_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"EMPTY\"\n            if crack_m & self.crack_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"PIT\"\n            return \"CRACK\"\n        if t == \"PIT\":\n            if pit_m & self.pit_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"EMPTY\"\n            return \"PIT\"\n        bit = self.destr_bit.get((x, y))\n        if bit and (destr_m & bit):\n            return \"EMPTY\"\n        return t\n\n    def plates_active(self, boxes):\n        if not self.plates_tuple:\n            return False\n        for p in self.plates_tuple:\n            if p not in boxes:\n                return False\n        return True\n\n    def is_high_wall(self, x, y, gf, p_act, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n            return True\n        t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n        if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n            return True\n        if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"P_GATE\" and not p_act:\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def blocks_oneway(self, x, y, cur_dir):\n        t = self.base[y][x]\n        if t in ONEWAY_TILES:\n            return t[-1] != cur_dir\n        return False\n\n    def get_laser_info(self, gf, boxes, tnts, destr_m):\n        \"\"\"Cached laser beam maps for period 3 and period 4.\"\"\"\n        if not self.has_lasers:\n            return None\n        p_act = self.plates_active(boxes)\n        key = (gf, p_act, boxes, tnts, destr_m)\n        cached = self.laser_cache.get(key)\n        if cached is not None:\n            return cached\n\n        red_cells = {}   # (x, y) -> bitmask of red cannon indices\n        blue_cells = {}  # (x, y) -> bitmask of blue cannon indices\n        red_tnts = set()\n        blue_tnts = set()\n        box_set = set(boxes)\n        tnt_set = set(tnts)\n\n        for idx, (lx, ly, period, d, lbit) in enumerate(self.lasers):\n            if destr_m & lbit:\n                continue\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n            cbit = 1 << idx\n            target_map = red_cells if period == 3 else blue_cells\n            target_tnts = red_tnts if period == 3 else blue_tnts\n            for dist in range(1, 11):\n                rx, ry = lx + dist * dx, ly + dist * dy\n                if not _in_bounds(rx, ry):\n                    break\n                if self.is_high_wall(rx, ry, gf, p_act, 0, 0, destr_m):\n                    break\n                if (rx, ry) in box_set:\n                    break\n                if (rx, ry) in tnt_set:\n                    target_tnts.add((rx, ry))\n                    break\n                target_map[(rx, ry)] = target_map.get((rx, ry), 0) | cbit\n\n        all_haz = set(red_cells.keys()) | set(blue_cells.keys())\n        res = (red_cells, blue_cells, red_tnts, blue_tnts, all_haz)\n        if len(self.laser_cache) < 20000:\n            self.laser_cache[key] = res\n        return res\n\n    def step(self, st, action):\n        \"\"\"\n        Exact v2.2 transition from WState st under action.\n        Returns (new_state_or_None, status, dmg_taken)\n        status in (\"RUNNING\", \"CLEARED\", \"DEAD\")\n        \"\"\"\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        if turn > MAX_TURNS:\n            return None, \"DEAD\", 0\n\n        box_set = set(boxes)\n        tnt_set = set(tnts)\n        exposed = {(hx, hy)}\n        armed_tnts = set()\n        bombs_list = list(active_bombs)\n        dmg_taken = 0\n        status = \"RUNNING\"\n\n        def cur_p_act():\n            if not self.plates_tuple:\n                return False\n            for p in self.plates_tuple:\n                if p not in box_set:\n                    return False\n            return True\n\n        def cur_hw(x, y):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                return True\n            t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n            if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n                return True\n            if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n                return True\n            if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n                return True\n            if t == \"P_GATE\" and not cur_p_act():\n                return True\n            return False\n\n        def can_box(x, y, d):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y) or cur_hw(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, d):\n                return False\n            t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n            if t in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                return False\n            if (x, y) in box_set or (x, y) in tnt_set or (x, y) == (hx, hy):\n                return False\n            return True\n\n        def can_hero(x, y, d):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y) or cur_hw(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, d):\n                return False\n            if (x, y) in box_set or (x, y) in tnt_set:\n                return False\n            return True\n\n        def displace_box(x0, y0, x1, y1, cur_dir):\n            nonlocal gf, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m\n            steps = 0\n            p_cnt = 0\n            sup = False\n            while True:\n                steps += 1\n                if self.tile_at(x0, y0, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m) == \"CRACK\":\n                    crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(x0, y0)]\n                box_set.discard((x0, y0))\n                box_set.add((x1, y1))\n                t1 = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                    box_set.discard((x1, y1))\n                    if self.base[y1][x1] == \"PIT\":\n                        pit_m |= self.pit_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    else:\n                        # broken CRACK filled by box -> EMPTY\n                        destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    break\n                elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                    gf = not gf\n\n                if steps >= 32:\n                    break\n                if sup:\n                    sup = False\n                    if p_cnt >= 2:\n                        break\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n\n                tc = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if tc == \"ICE\":\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n                elif tc in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[tc][cur_dir]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n                elif tc in SPRING_TILES:\n                    cur_dir = tc[-1]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    blk = None\n                    for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                        pkx, pky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                        if not can_box(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                            blk = k\n                            if _in_bounds(pkx, pky) and (pkx, pky) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                                armed_tnts.add((pkx, pky))\n                            break\n                    if blk == 1:\n                        break\n                    lk = (blk - 1) if blk is not None else 3\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, x1 + lk * dx, y1 + lk * dy\n                    continue\n                elif tc in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[tc])\n                    if pp is None or pp in box_set or pp in tnt_set or pp == (hx, hy):\n                        break\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                    sup = True\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, pp[0], pp[1]\n                    continue\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        def displace_hero(x1, y1, cur_dir):\n            nonlocal hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, crack_m, destr_m, dmg_taken, status\n            x0, y0 = hx, hy\n            steps = 0\n            p_cnt = 0\n            sup = False\n            stop_m = False\n            while True:\n                steps += 1\n                if self.tile_at(x0, y0, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m) == \"CRACK\":\n                    crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(x0, y0)]\n                hx, hy = x1, y1\n                exposed.add((hx, hy))\n                t1 = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                    hp -= 100\n                    dmg_taken += 100\n                elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                    hp -= 45\n                    dmg_taken += 45\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    stop_m = True\n                elif t1 == \"HEAL\":\n                    hp = min(100, hp + 30)\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                    gf = not gf\n\n                if hp <= 0:\n                    status = \"DEAD\"\n                    return\n\n                if t1 == \"K1\":\n                    kmask |= 1\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"K2\":\n                    kmask |= 2\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"V\" and kmask == 3 and hp > 0:\n                    status = \"CLEARED\"\n                    return\n\n                if steps >= 32 or stop_m:\n                    break\n                if sup:\n                    sup = False\n                    if p_cnt >= 2:\n                        break\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n\n                tc = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if tc == \"ICE\":\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n                elif tc in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[tc][cur_dir]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n                elif tc in SPRING_TILES:\n                    cur_dir = tc[-1]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    blk = None\n                    for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                        pkx, pky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                        if not can_hero(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                            blk = k\n                            break\n                    if blk == 1:\n                        break\n                    lk = (blk - 1) if blk is not None else 3\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, x1 + lk * dx, y1 + lk * dy\n                    continue\n                elif tc in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[tc])\n                    if pp is None or pp in box_set or pp in tnt_set:\n                        break\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                    sup = True\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, pp[0], pp[1]\n                    continue\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        # Phase A\n        if action == \"WAIT\":\n            pass\n        elif action == \"BOMB\":\n            if bombs_left > 0 and not any(b[0] == hx and b[1] == hy for b in bombs_list):\n                bombs_left -= 1\n                bombs_list.append((hx, hy, 3))\n        elif action.startswith(\"THROW_\"):\n            if throws > 0:\n                throws -= 1\n                sx, sy = hx, hy\n                cd = action[-1]\n                rng = 6\n                sp = 0\n                shat = False\n                stop_p = False\n                while rng > 0:\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                    sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                    rng -= 1\n                    if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or cur_hw(sx, sy) or (sx, sy) in box_set:\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if (sx, sy) in tnt_set:\n                        armed_tnts.add((sx, sy))\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    t = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                    if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                        gf = not gf\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                        destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(sx, sy)]\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                    elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                        pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                        if pp is not None and pp not in box_set and pp not in tnt_set:\n                            sx, sy = pp\n                            sp += 1\n                            if sp == 2:\n                                stop_p = True\n                                break\n                if not shat and not stop_p and rng == 0:\n                    t6 = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                    if t6 == \"ICE\" or t6 in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        for _ in range(32):\n                            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                            sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                            if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or cur_hw(sx, sy) or (sx, sy) in box_set:\n                                break\n                            if (sx, sy) in tnt_set:\n                                armed_tnts.add((sx, sy))\n                                break\n                            t = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                            if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                                gf = not gf\n                                break\n                            if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                                destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(sx, sy)]\n                                break\n                            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                                cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                            elif t == \"ICE\":\n                                continue\n                            elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                                pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                                if pp is not None and pp not in box_set and pp not in tnt_set:\n                                    sx, sy = pp\n                                    sp += 1\n                                    if sp == 2:\n                                        break\n                                else:\n                                    break\n                            else:\n                                break\n        elif action.startswith(\"MOVE_\"):\n            d = action[-1]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n            p1x, p1y = hx + dx, hy + dy\n            if _in_bounds(p1x, p1y) and not cur_hw(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d):\n                if (p1x, p1y) in box_set:\n                    p2x, p2y = hx + 2 * dx, hy + 2 * dy\n                    if can_box(p2x, p2y, d):\n                        displace_box(p1x, p1y, p2x, p2y, d)\n                        if can_hero(p1x, p1y, d):\n                            displace_hero(p1x, p1y, d)\n                    elif _in_bounds(p2x, p2y) and (p2x, p2y) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(p2x, p2y, d):\n                        armed_tnts.add((p2x, p2y))\n                elif (p1x, p1y) in tnt_set:\n                    pass\n                else:\n                    displace_hero(p1x, p1y, d)\n        elif action.startswith(\"JUMP_\"):\n            if jumps > 0:\n                jumps -= 1\n                d = action[-1]\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n                p1x, p1y = hx + dx, hy + dy\n                p2x, p2y = hx + 2 * dx, hy + 2 * dy\n                p1_ok = _in_bounds(p1x, p1y) and not cur_hw(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d)\n                if p1_ok and can_hero(p2x, p2y, d):\n                    displace_hero(p2x, p2y, d)\n\n        if status != \"RUNNING\":\n            ns = (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n                  tuple(sorted(box_set)), tuple(sorted(tnt_set)),\n                  crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, tuple(bombs_list), turn + 1)\n            return ns, status, dmg_taken\n\n        def detonate(seeds):\n            nonlocal hp, dmg_taken, crack_m, destr_m, status\n            exploded = set()\n            q = collections.deque(seeds)\n            while q:\n                ex, ey = q.popleft()\n                if (ex, ey) in exploded:\n                    continue\n                exploded.add((ex, ey))\n                tnt_set.discard((ex, ey))\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = ex + dx, ey + dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            continue\n                        if (nx, ny) == (hx, hy):\n                            hp -= 60\n                            dmg_taken += 60\n                        box_set.discard((nx, ny))\n                        if (nx, ny) in tnt_set:\n                            tnt_set.discard((nx, ny))\n                            if (nx, ny) not in exploded:\n                                q.append((nx, ny))\n                        nt = self.tile_at(nx, ny, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                        if nt == \"CRACK\":\n                            crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(nx, ny)]\n                        elif nt in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY:\n                            destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(nx, ny)]\n            if hp <= 0:\n                status = \"DEAD\"\n\n        # Phase C\n        det_seeds = set(armed_tnts)\n        next_bombs = []\n        for bx, by, bt in bombs_list:\n            bt -= 1\n            if bt <= 0:\n                det_seeds.add((bx, by))\n            else:\n                next_bombs.append((bx, by, bt))\n        if det_seeds:\n            detonate(det_seeds)\n        if status != \"RUNNING\":\n            return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n\n        new_boxes = tuple(sorted(box_set))\n        new_tnts = tuple(sorted(tnt_set))\n\n        # Phase D\n        if self.has_lasers and (turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0):\n            linfo = self.get_laser_info(gf, new_boxes, new_tnts, destr_m)\n            red_cells, blue_cells, red_tnts, blue_tnts, _ = linfo\n            hit_mask = 0\n            ltnts = set()\n            if turn % 3 == 0:\n                ltnts |= red_tnts\n                for c in exposed:\n                    m = red_cells.get(c, 0)\n                    if m:\n                        hit_mask |= m\n            if turn % 4 == 0:\n                ltnts |= blue_tnts\n                for c in exposed:\n                    m = blue_cells.get(c, 0)\n                    if m:\n                        hit_mask |= m\n            if hit_mask:\n                hits = bin(hit_mask).count(\"1\")\n                hp -= 50 * hits\n                dmg_taken += 50 * hits\n                if hp <= 0:\n                    return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n            if ltnts:\n                detonate(ltnts)\n                if status != \"RUNNING\":\n                    return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n                new_boxes = tuple(sorted(box_set))\n                new_tnts = tuple(sorted(tnt_set))\n\n        ns = (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n              new_boxes, new_tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, tuple(next_bombs), turn + 1)\n        return ns, \"RUNNING\", dmg_taken\n\n    def _is_box_deadlocked(self, boxes):\n        if not self.plates_tuple:\n            return False\n        alive = 0\n        for b in boxes:\n            if b in self.plates_set:\n                alive += 1\n            elif b not in self.dead_box_cells:\n                alive += 1\n        return alive < len(self.plates_tuple)\n\n    def heuristic(self, st):\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        # Unfilled plates matching\n        h = 0\n        unfilled = [i for i, p in enumerate(self.plates_tuple) if p not in boxes]\n        if unfilled:\n            free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if b not in self.plates_set]\n            # Greedy/exact small matching using Manhattan or BFS dist\n            if len(unfilled) <= len(free_boxes):\n                # Best lower-bound matching\n                used = set()\n                nearest_box_d = 999\n                for pi in unfilled:\n                    px, py = self.plates_tuple[pi]\n                    best_d = 999\n                    best_bi = -1\n                    for bi, (bx, by) in enumerate(free_boxes):\n                        if bi in used:\n                            continue\n                        d = self.dist_plates[pi][by][bx]\n                        if d >= 999:\n                            d = abs(bx - px) + abs(by - py)\n                        if d < best_d:\n                            best_d = d\n                            best_bi = bi\n                    if best_bi >= 0:\n                        used.add(best_bi)\n                        h += best_d * 2 + 6\n                        bx, by = free_boxes[best_bi]\n                        hd = abs(hx - bx) + abs(hy - by)\n                        if hd < nearest_box_d:\n                            nearest_box_d = hd\n                if nearest_box_d < 999:\n                    h += nearest_box_d\n            else:\n                return 99999\n\n        # Keys + V distance\n        k1x, k1y = self.k1_pos\n        k2x, k2y = self.k2_pos\n        vx, vy = self.v_pos\n        d_k1 = self.dist_k1[hy][hx]\n        if d_k1 >= 999:\n            d_k1 = abs(hx - k1x) + abs(hy - k1y)\n        d_k2 = self.dist_k2[hy][hx]\n        if d_k2 >= 999:\n            d_k2 = abs(hx - k2x) + abs(hy - k2y)\n        d_v = self.dist_v[hy][hx]\n        if d_v >= 999:\n            d_v = abs(hx - vx) + abs(hy - vy)\n        d_k1_k2 = abs(k1x - k2x) + abs(k1y - k2y)\n        d_k1_v = abs(k1x - vx) + abs(k1y - vy)\n        d_k2_v = abs(k2x - vx) + abs(k2y - vy)\n\n        if kmask == 0:\n            h += min(d_k1 + d_k1_k2 + d_k2_v, d_k2 + d_k1_k2 + d_k1_v) + 20\n        elif kmask == 1:\n            h += d_k2 + d_k2_v + 10\n        elif kmask == 2:\n            h += d_k1 + d_k1_v + 10\n        else:\n            h += d_v\n\n        return h\n\n    def expand_macros(self, st, deadline):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run a fast Micro-Dijkstra moving ONLY the hero (MOVE/JUMP/WAIT),\n        discovering all reachable Macro-Actions:\n          - CLEAR / PICKUP_K1 / PICKUP_K2 / FLIP_SWITCH / HEAL / MIMIC-pass\n          - PUSH_BOX (all reachable adjacent boxes & valid directions)\n          - THROW (all reachable useful throw spots)\n          - BOMB + 2-turn safe escape (all reachable useful bomb spots)\n        Returns (cleared_actions_or_None, list_of_(next_state, action_seq, dmg_total))\n        \"\"\"\n        (sx, sy, shp, skmask, sgf, sjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n         sboxes, stnts, scrack_m, spit_m, sdestr_m, sactive_bombs, sturn) = st\n\n        if sactive_bombs:\n            # Should not normally happen in macro states, handle 1 primitive step\n            out = []\n            for act in (\"MOVE_R\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_L\", \"WAIT\", \"JUMP_R\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_L\"):\n                ns, status, dmg = self.step(st, act)\n                if status == \"CLEARED\":\n                    return [act], []\n                if status == \"RUNNING\":\n                    out.append((ns, [act], dmg))\n            return None, out\n\n        box_set = set(sboxes)\n        tnt_set = set(stnts)\n        p_act = self.plates_active(sboxes)\n\n        linfo = self.get_laser_info(sgf, sboxes, stnts, sdestr_m) if self.has_lasers else None\n        all_haz = linfo[4] if linfo else set()\n        red_cells = linfo[0] if linfo else {}\n        blue_cells = linfo[1] if linfo else {}\n\n        # Precompute cells where WAIT is useful (adjacent to laser hazard or ice/spring/mirror/portal)\n        wait_worthy = set()\n        if self.has_lasers and all_haz:\n            for (lx, ly) in all_haz:\n                for dy in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):\n                    for dx in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):\n                        if abs(dx) + abs(dy) <= 2:\n                            wait_worthy.add((lx + dx, ly + dy))\n            wait_worthy.add((sx, sy))\n\n        # Micro-Dijkstra state: (x, y, tmod, jumps_left, crack_m) -> (dmg, steps)\n        # Priority: (dmg * 500 + steps * 10 + (sjumps - jumps_left) * 2)\n        tmod0 = (sturn % 12) if self.has_lasers else 0\n        pq = [(0, 0, 0, sx, sy, shp, sjumps, scrack_m, sturn, ())]\n        best_micro = {(sx, sy, tmod0, sjumps, scrack_m): (0, 0)}\n        # Track best arrival per (x, y, tmod) for macro triggers (don't duplicate throws/bombs across all 12 tmods unless needed!)\n        best_cell_arrival = {}  # (x, y) -> (dmg, steps, state_at_cell, path)\n        best_push_arrival = {}  # (bx, by, d) -> (dmg, steps, state_at_cell, path)\n        best_bomb_arrival = {}  # (x, y) -> (dmg, steps, state_at_cell, path)\n        emitted_events = {}     # event_dedup_key -> (dmg, steps, ns, path)\n\n        # Quick check: cells adjacent to boxes for push triggers\n        push_spots = collections.defaultdict(list)\n        for (bx, by) in sboxes:\n            for d, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                px, py = bx - dx, by - dy\n                if _in_bounds(px, py):\n                    push_spots[(px, py)].append((bx, by, d))\n\n        pops = 0\n        max_pops = 2200 if self.has_lasers else 1200\n\n        while pq and pops < max_pops:\n            prio, cdmg, csteps, cx, cy, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cpath = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            pops += 1\n            if pops & 127 == 0 and time.perf_counter() > deadline:\n                break\n            if cturn > MAX_TURNS:\n                continue\n\n            ctmod = (cturn % 12) if self.has_lasers else 0\n            mkey = (cx, cy, ctmod, cjumps, ccm)\n            prev = best_micro.get(mkey)\n            if prev is not None and (cdmg, csteps) > prev:\n                continue\n\n            # Record arrival at (cx, cy) for THROW / BOMB / PUSH\n            cur_full_st = (\n                cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn\n            )\n            prev_ca = best_cell_arrival.get((cx, cy))\n            if prev_ca is None or (cdmg, csteps) < (prev_ca[0], prev_ca[1]):\n                best_cell_arrival[(cx, cy)] = (cdmg, csteps, cur_full_st, cpath)\n\n            # Check BOX pushes from (cx, cy)\n            if (cx, cy) in push_spots:\n                for (bx, by, d) in push_spots[(cx, cy)]:\n                    pkey = (bx, by, d, ctmod if (bx, by) in all_haz or (cx, cy) in all_haz else 0)\n                    if pkey not in best_push_arrival:\n                        best_push_arrival[pkey] = (cdmg, csteps, cur_full_st, cpath, d)\n\n            # Check BOMB from (cx, cy)\n            if sbombs > 0 and (cx, cy) in self.bomb_candidates:\n                bkey = (cx, cy)\n                if bkey not in best_bomb_arrival:\n                    best_bomb_arrival[bkey] = (cdmg, csteps, cur_full_st, cpath)\n\n            # Generate micro moves: MOVE_*, JUMP_*, WAIT\n            # 1. WAIT (only if lasers exist and at wait_worthy or near ice/spring/mirror/portal)\n            if self.has_lasers and (cx, cy) in wait_worthy and csteps < 120:\n                # Quick laser check for WAIT\n                w_hit = 0\n                if cturn % 3 == 0:\n                    w_hit |= red_cells.get((cx, cy), 0)\n                if cturn % 4 == 0:\n                    w_hit |= blue_cells.get((cx, cy), 0)\n                wdmg = 50 * bin(w_hit).count(\"1\") if w_hit else 0\n                if chp - wdmg > 0:\n                    nturn = cturn + 1\n                    ntmod = nturn % 12\n                    ndmg = cdmg + wdmg\n                    nsteps = csteps + 1\n                    nk = (cx, cy, ntmod, cjumps, ccm)\n                    pb = best_micro.get(nk)\n                    if pb is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < pb:\n                        best_micro[nk] = (ndmg, nsteps)\n                        nprio = ndmg * 500 + nsteps * 10 + (sjumps - cjumps) * 3\n                        heapq.heappush(pq, (nprio, ndmg, nsteps, cx, cy, chp - wdmg, cjumps, ccm, nturn, cpath + (\"WAIT\",)))\n\n            # 2. MOVE_* and JUMP_*\n            for d in DIR_LIST:\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n                p1x, p1y = cx + dx, cy + dy\n                if not _in_bounds(p1x, p1y):\n                    continue\n                if not self.is_high_wall(p1x, p1y, sgf, p_act, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d):\n                    if (p1x, p1y) not in box_set and (p1x, p1y) not in tnt_set:\n                        act = f\"MOVE_{d}\"\n                        ns, nstatus, step_dmg = self.step(cur_full_st, act)\n                        if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                            return list(cpath) + [act], []\n                        if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                            (nx, ny, nhp, nkm, ngf, nj, _, _, _, _, ncm, _, ndm, _, nturn) = ns\n                            ndmg = cdmg + step_dmg\n                            nsteps = csteps + 1\n                            # If world state changed (key, switch, heal, mimic), emit as macro event!\n                            if nkm != skmask or ngf != sgf or ndm != sdestr_m:\n                                ekey = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, ncm, ndm, nturn % 12 if self.has_lasers else 0)\n                                pe = emitted_events.get(ekey)\n                                if pe is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < (pe[0], pe[1]):\n                                    emitted_events[ekey] = (ndmg, nsteps, ns, cpath + (act,))\n                            else:\n                                ntmod = (nturn % 12) if self.has_lasers else 0\n                                nk = (nx, ny, ntmod, nj, ncm)\n                                pb = best_micro.get(nk)\n                                if pb is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < pb:\n                                    best_micro[nk] = (ndmg, nsteps)\n                                    nprio = ndmg * 500 + nsteps * 10 + (sjumps - nj) * 3\n                                    heapq.heappush(pq, (nprio, ndmg, nsteps, nx, ny, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cpath + (act,)))\n\n                # JUMP_*\n                if cjumps > 0:\n                    p2x, p2y = cx + 2 * dx, cy + 2 * dy\n                    if not _in_bounds(p2x, p2y):\n                        continue\n                    t1 = self.tile_at(p1x, p1y, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m)\n                    # Only jump over meaningful obstacles/hazards (PIT, CRACK, BOX, TNT, MIMIC, SWITCH, laser cell, ICE, SPRING, MIRROR, PORTAL, ONEWAY)\n                    jump_useful = (\n                        t1 != \"EMPTY\"\n                        or (p1x, p1y) in box_set\n                        or (p1x, p1y) in tnt_set\n                        or (p1x, p1y) in all_haz\n                    )\n                    if not jump_useful:\n                        continue\n                    if (not self.is_high_wall(p1x, p1y, sgf, p_act, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m)\n                            and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d)\n                            and not self.is_high_wall(p2x, p2y, sgf, p_act, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m)\n                            and not self.blocks_oneway(p2x, p2y, d)\n                            and (p2x, p2y) not in box_set and (p2x, p2y) not in tnt_set):\n                        act = f\"JUMP_{d}\"\n                        ns, nstatus, step_dmg = self.step(cur_full_st, act)\n                        if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                            return list(cpath) + [act], []\n                        if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                            (nx, ny, nhp, nkm, ngf, nj, _, _, _, _, ncm, _, ndm, _, nturn) = ns\n                            ndmg = cdmg + step_dmg\n                            nsteps = csteps + 1\n                            if nkm != skmask or ngf != sgf or ndm != sdestr_m:\n                                ekey = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, ncm, ndm, nturn % 12 if self.has_lasers else 0)\n                                pe = emitted_events.get(ekey)\n                                if pe is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < (pe[0], pe[1]):\n                                    emitted_events[ekey] = (ndmg, nsteps, ns, cpath + (act,))\n                            else:\n                                ntmod = (nturn % 12) if self.has_lasers else 0\n                                nk = (nx, ny, ntmod, nj, ncm)\n                                pb = best_micro.get(nk)\n                                if pb is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < pb:\n                                    best_micro[nk] = (ndmg, nsteps)\n                                    nprio = ndmg * 500 + nsteps * 10 + (sjumps - nj) * 3\n                                    heapq.heappush(pq, (nprio, ndmg, nsteps, nx, ny, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cpath + (act,)))\n\n        macros = []\n        # 1. Add emitted events (keys, switches, heals, mimic passes)\n        for (dmg, steps, ns, path) in emitted_events.values():\n            macros.append((ns, list(path), dmg))\n\n        # 2. Add BOX push transitions\n        for (_, _, cur_st, path, d) in best_push_arrival.values():\n            act = f\"MOVE_{d}\"\n            ns, nstatus, sdmg = self.step(cur_st, act)\n            if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                return list(path) + [act], []\n            if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                # Only keep if box/tnt/gates/pits actually changed!\n                if ns[8] != cur_st[8] or ns[9] != cur_st[9] or ns[4] != cur_st[4] or ns[11] != cur_st[11] or ns[12] != cur_st[12]:\n                    if not self._is_box_deadlocked(ns[8]):\n                        tot_dmg = (shp - ns[2]) if shp >= ns[2] else 0\n                        macros.append((ns, list(path) + [act], tot_dmg))\n\n        # 3. Add useful THROW transitions (both static-table AND dynamic TNT line-of-sight!)\n        if sthrows > 0:\n            # Find cells with direct LOS to any live TNT (within 6 cells)\n            tnt_throw_spots = collections.defaultdict(list)\n            for (tx, ty) in stnts:\n                for d, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                    opp = {\"U\": \"D\", \"D\": \"U\", \"L\": \"R\", \"R\": \"L\"}[d]\n                    for dist in range(1, 7):\n                        rx, ry = tx + dist * dx, ty + dist * dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(rx, ry):\n                            break\n                        if self.is_high_wall(rx, ry, sgf, p_act, scrack_m, spit_m, sdestr_m) or (rx, ry) in box_set:\n                            break\n                        tnt_throw_spots[(rx, ry)].append(opp)\n\n            seen_throw_effect = set()\n            for (cx, cy), (cdmg, csteps, cur_st, path) in best_cell_arrival.items():\n                dirs_to_try = set(self.throw_candidates.get((cx, cy), ())) | set(tnt_throw_spots.get((cx, cy), ()))\n                for d in dirs_to_try:\n                    act = f\"THROW_{d}\"\n                    ns, nstatus, sdmg = self.step(cur_st, act)\n                    if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                        if ns[4] != cur_st[4] or ns[9] != cur_st[9] or ns[12] != cur_st[12]:\n                            eff_key = (ns[4], ns[9], ns[12])\n                            if eff_key not in seen_throw_effect:\n                                seen_throw_effect.add(eff_key)\n                                tot_dmg = max(0, shp - ns[2])\n                                macros.append((ns, list(path) + [act], tot_dmg))\n\n        # 4. Add useful BOMB + 2-turn safe escape transitions (top candidates)\n        if sbombs > 0 and best_bomb_arrival:\n            # Score bomb spots by how valuable their 3x3 destruction is\n            scored_bombs = []\n            for (bx, by), (cdmg, csteps, cur_st, path) in best_bomb_arrival.items():\n                val = 0\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = bx + dx, by + dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            continue\n                        if (nx, ny) in tnt_set:\n                            val += 50\n                        if (nx, ny) in box_set and (nx, ny) in self.plates_set:\n                            val += 15\n                        nt = self.tile_at(nx, ny, cur_st[10], spit_m, sdestr_m)\n                        if nt in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                            val += 40\n                        elif nt in (\"R_GATE\", \"B_GATE\"):\n                            val += 35\n                        elif nt == \"MIMIC\":\n                            val += 25\n                        elif nt in SPRING_TILES or nt in MIRROR_MAP:\n                            val += 15\n                        elif nt == \"W\":\n                            # Bonus if near K1, K2, V, or separates empty spaces\n                            val += 10\n                            if (abs(nx - self.k1_pos[0]) + abs(ny - self.k1_pos[1]) <= 3\n                                    or abs(nx - self.k2_pos[0]) + abs(ny - self.k2_pos[1]) <= 3\n                                    or abs(nx - self.v_pos[0]) + abs(ny - self.v_pos[1]) <= 3):\n                                val += 40\n                if val > 0:\n                    scored_bombs.append((-val, csteps, bx, by, cur_st, path))\n            scored_bombs.sort()\n\n            seen_bomb_effect = set()\n            for _, _, bx, by, cur_st, path in scored_bombs[:28]:\n                if time.perf_counter() > deadline:\n                    break\n                st1, s1, _ = self.step(cur_st, \"BOMB\")\n                if s1 != \"RUNNING\":\n                    continue\n                # Find 2-step safe escape outside 3x3 of (bx, by)\n                best_esc = None\n                esc_actions = (\"MOVE_R\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"JUMP_R\", \"JUMP_L\", \"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_D\", \"WAIT\")\n                for a1 in esc_actions:\n                    if a1.startswith(\"JUMP_\") and st1[5] <= 0:\n                        continue\n                    st2, s2, _ = self.step(st1, a1)\n                    if s2 == \"CLEARED\":\n                        return list(path) + [\"BOMB\", a1], []\n                    if s2 != \"RUNNING\":\n                        continue\n                    for a2 in esc_actions:\n                        if a2.startswith(\"JUMP_\") and st2[5] <= 0:\n                            continue\n                        st3, s3, _ = self.step(st2, a2)\n                        if s3 == \"CLEARED\":\n                            return list(path) + [\"BOMB\", a1, a2], []\n                        if s3 != \"RUNNING\":\n                            continue\n                        # Check bomb actually detonated (active_bombs empty) and didn't lose too much HP\n                        if not st3[13] and st3[2] >= cur_st[2] - 50:\n                            # Prefer 0 damage, no jumps spent\n                            sc = (cur_st[2] - st3[2]) * 100 + (cur_st[5] - st3[5]) * 10\n                            if best_esc is None or sc < best_esc[0]:\n                                best_esc = (sc, st3, [a1, a2])\n                                if sc == 0:\n                                    break\n                    if best_esc is not None and best_esc[0] == 0:\n                        break\n                if best_esc is not None:\n                    _, st3, esc2 = best_esc\n                    # Check it actually destroyed something\n                    if st3[12] != cur_st[12] or st3[8] != cur_st[8] or st3[9] != cur_st[9]:\n                        eff_key = (st3[12], st3[8], st3[9], st3[10])\n                        if eff_key not in seen_bomb_effect:\n                            seen_bomb_effect.add(eff_key)\n                            tot_dmg = max(0, shp - st3[2])\n                            macros.append((st3, list(path) + [\"BOMB\"] + esc2, tot_dmg))\n\n        return None, macros\n\n    def solve(self, init_state, budget_sec=1.45):\n        \"\"\"\n        Weighted Macro-Action A* solver.\n        Returns (plan_actions_list_or_None, best_partial_actions, stats_dict)\n        \"\"\"\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\n        deadline = t0 + budget_sec\n\n        # Pass 1: Weighted Macro-A* (w=2.0)\n        plan, partial, nodes = self._run_macro_astar(init_state, deadline, weight=2.2)\n        if plan is not None:\n            # Verify plan length <= remaining turns\n            if len(plan) <= MAX_TURNS - init_state[14] + 1:\n                return plan, partial, {\"solved\": True, \"nodes\": nodes, \"elapsed\": time.perf_counter() - t0, \"len\": len(plan)}\n\n        # Pass 2: If time remains, try greedier weight (w=4.5)\n        if time.perf_counter() < deadline - 0.15:\n            plan2, partial2, nodes2 = self._run_macro_astar(init_state, deadline, weight=4.5)\n            if plan2 is not None and len(plan2) <= MAX_TURNS - init_state[14] + 1:\n                return plan2, partial2, {\"solved\": True, \"nodes\": nodes + nodes2, \"elapsed\": time.perf_counter() - t0, \"len\": len(plan2)}\n            if partial2 and len(partial2) > len(partial):\n                partial = partial2\n\n        return None, partial, {\"solved\": False, \"nodes\": nodes, \"elapsed\": time.perf_counter() - t0}\n\n    def _run_macro_astar(self, init_state, deadline, weight=2.2):\n        def state_key(st):\n            tmod = (st[14] % 12) if self.has_lasers else 0\n            return (st[0], st[1], st[3], st[4], st[5], st[6], st[7],\n                    st[8], st[9], st[10], st[11], st[12], st[13], tmod)\n\n        def progress_rank(st):\n            km = st[3]\n            keys_cnt = (1 if km & 1 else 0) + (1 if km & 2 else 0)\n            p_filled = sum(1 for p in self.plates_tuple if p in st[8])\n            return (keys_cnt, p_filled, 1 if self.plates_active(st[8]) else 0, -self.heuristic(st), st[2])\n\n        h0 = self.heuristic(init_state)\n        pq = [(weight * h0, 0, 0, init_state)]\n        parents = {0: (None, [])}  # node_id -> (parent_id, actions)\n        best_g = {state_key(init_state): (0, init_state[2])}  # key -> (g_cost, hp)\n        best_partial_rank = progress_rank(init_state)\n        best_partial_id = 0\n        next_id = 1\n        expanded = 0\n\n        def reconstruct(nid):\n            chunks = []\n            cur = nid\n            while cur is not None:\n                pid, acts = parents[cur]\n                if acts:\n                    chunks.append(acts)\n                cur = pid\n            chunks.reverse()\n            out = []\n            for ch in chunks:\n                out.extend(ch)\n            return out\n\n        while pq and time.perf_counter() < deadline:\n            f, g, nid, st = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            sk = state_key(st)\n            bg = best_g.get(sk)\n            if bg is not None and g > bg[0] and st[2] <= bg[1]:\n                continue\n\n            expanded += 1\n            pr = progress_rank(st)\n            if pr > best_partial_rank:\n                best_partial_rank = pr\n                best_partial_id = nid\n\n            cleared_acts, macros = self.expand_macros(st, deadline)\n            if cleared_acts is not None:\n                full_plan = reconstruct(nid) + cleared_acts\n                return full_plan, full_plan, expanded\n\n            for ns, act_seq, step_dmg in macros:\n                if ns[14] > MAX_TURNS + 1:\n                    continue\n                # Cost: turns + heavy damage penalty (50 HP = 120 turns in score!)\n                ng = g + len(act_seq) + max(0, st[2] - ns[2]) * 2.5\n                nsk = state_key(ns)\n                nbg = best_g.get(nsk)\n                if nbg is not None and ng >= nbg[0] and ns[2] <= nbg[1]:\n                    continue\n                best_g[nsk] = (ng, ns[2])\n                cid = next_id\n                next_id += 1\n                parents[cid] = (nid, act_seq)\n                nh = self.heuristic(ns)\n                heapq.heappush(pq, (ng + weight * nh, ng, cid, ns))\n\n        return None, reconstruct(best_partial_id), expanded\n",

## 2026-11-20T01:10:43.497000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=201 message=msg_1f49319a0001EE0XDPcVF4s5yT

**Analyzing Performance Bottleneck**

I've zeroed in on the `expand_macros` function. The profiling output is glaring; a single call is eating up a huge chunk of time – roughly 75ms per macro node. This needs immediate investigation. What's causing this bottleneck within the macro expansion process? I'm digging into `expand_macros` to understand why.


**Pinpointing Optimization Targets**

I've uncovered some egregious inefficiencies within `expand_macros`. The profiling has unveiled a costly `self.step()` call, inside a loop. This is being executed 17,600 times per macro node, due to redundant set creations and tuple rebuilds. This appears to be a major performance killer. Furthermore, the `BOMB` escape path is also triggering the `self.step()` method a large number of times. It looks like Micro-BFS is exploding on (x, y, tmod, jumps).


**Refining Micro-BFS Strategy**

I've made a crucial breakthrough in optimizing Micro-BFS! I realize that most cells don't need the complexity of `tmod` or `jumps` calculations. If a cell isn't near a hazard (laser, ICE, SPRING, MIRROR, PORTAL), the `tmod_key` can be set to 0. Also, I'm now tracking `best_micro_no_jump` to prune redundant expansions, eliminating unnecessary `jumps_left` iterations. Crucially, I've realized normal moves onto empty cells don't even need `self.step()`!


**Optimizing Terrain Representation**

I've had a significant insight on optimizing the Micro-BFS. It appears pre-computing a flat array representing the terrain state, along with `is_blocked` and laser bitmasks, is highly effective, which gets me an array lookup and a laser check. This eliminates redundant function calls, sets, tuples, and closures during the bulk of Micro-BFS steps. These optimizations are expected to boost performance by a factor of 100x, bringing the entire 912-cell grid well under 0.3ms! Also, I have also identified that on level `P1:OFFICIAL_02`, the timeout occurs, due to the number of walls, and now can be further analyzed.
