else:\n        for pos in possible_mimics:\n            mimics.add(pos)\n    return mimics, heals\n\n\nclass FastWorld:\n    \"\"\"Compact-bitmask exact v2.2 physics + ultra-fast flat-grid Micro-BFS Macro-A*.\"\"\"\n\n    def build_from_state(self, state):\n        obs_t = state[\"terrain\"]\n        obs_o = state[\"occupant\"]\n        clues = state[\"clues\"]\n        mimics, heals = _classify_chests_exact(obs_t, clues)\n\n        self.base = [row[:] for row in obs_t]\n        for mx, my in mimics:\n            self.base[my][mx] = \"MIMIC\"\n        for hx, hy in heals:\n            self.base[hy][hx] = \"HEAL\"\n\n        self.base_flat = [self.base[y][x] for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT) for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)]\n        self.plates = []\n        self.pgates = []\n        self.switches = []\n        self.portals = {}\n        self.lasers = []\n        self.crack_bit = {}\n        self.crack_cells = []\n        self.pit_bit = {}\n        self.pit_cells = []\n        self.destr_bit = {}\n        self.destr_cells = []\n        self.heals = []\n        self.mimics = []\n\n        c_cnt = p_cnt = d_cnt = 0\n        init_boxes = []\n        init_tnts = []\n\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n                t = self.base[y][x]\n                occ = obs_o[y][x]\n                if occ == \"BOX\":\n                    init_boxes.append((x, y))\n                elif occ == \"TNT\":\n                    init_tnts.append((x, y))\n\n                if t == \"PLATE\":\n                    self.plates.append((x, y))\n                elif t == \"P_GATE\":\n                    self.pgates.append((x, y))\n                elif t == \"SWITCH\":\n                    self.switches.append((x, y))\n                elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    self.portals[t] = (x, y)\n                elif t == \"CRACK\":\n                    bit = 1 << c_cnt\n                    self.crack_bit[(x, y)] = bit\n                    self.crack_cells.append((x, y, y * 40 + x, bit))\n                    c_cnt += 1\n                    dbit = 1 << d_cnt\n                    self.destr_bit[(x, y)] = dbit\n                    self.destr_cells.append((x, y, y * 40 + x, dbit))\n                    d_cnt += 1\n                elif t == \"PIT\":\n                    bit = 1 << p_cnt\n                    self.pit_bit[(x, y)] = bit\n                    self.pit_cells.append((x, y, y * 40 + x, bit))\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                elif t == \"HEAL\":\n                    self.heals.append((x, y))\n                elif t == \"MIMIC\":\n                    self.mimics.append((x, y))\n\n                if t in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY or t in (\"K1\", \"K2\"):\n                    if (x, y) not in self.destr_bit:\n                        dbit = 1 << d_cnt\n                        self.destr_bit[(x, y)] = dbit\n                        self.destr_cells.append((x, y, y * 40 + x, dbit))\n                        d_cnt += 1\n\n                if t in LASER1_TILES:\n                    self.lasers.append((x, y, 3, t[-1], self.destr_bit[(x, y)]))\n                elif t in LASER2_TILES:\n                    self.lasers.append((x, y, 4, t[-1], self.destr_bit[(x, y)]))\n\n        self.plates_tuple = tuple(self.plates)\n        self.plates_set = frozenset(self.plates)\n        self.has_lasers = len(self.lasers) > 0\n        self.targets = state[\"targets\"]\n        self.k1_pos = tuple(self.targets[\"K1\"])\n        self.k2_pos = tuple(self.targets[\"K2\"])\n        self.v_pos = tuple(self.targets[\"V\"])\n\n        self.laser_cache = {}\n        self._precompute_static()\n\n        hero = state[\"hero\"]\n        kmask = (1 if hero[\"keys\"][0] else 0) | (2 if hero[\"keys\"][1] else 0)\n        init_destr = 0\n        if hero[\"keys\"][0] and self.k1_pos in self.destr_bit:\n            init_destr |= self.destr_bit[self.k1_pos]\n        if hero[\"keys\"][1] and self.k2_pos in self.destr_bit:\n            init_destr |= self.destr_bit[self.k2_pos]\n        init_bombs = tuple((b[\"x\"], b[\"y\"], b[\"timer\"]) for b in state.get(\"bombs\", []))\n\n        return (\n            hero[\"x\"], hero[\"y\"], hero[\"hp\"], kmask,\n            bool(state.get(\"gates_flipped\", False)),\n            hero[\"jumps\"], hero[\"throws\"], hero[\"bombs\"],\n            tuple(sorted(init_boxes)),\n            tuple(sorted(init_tnts)),\n            0, 0, init_destr,\n            init_bombs,\n            state[\"turn\"],\n        )\n\n    def _precompute_static(self):\n        def bfs_dist(target_xy):\n            dist = [[999] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n            tx, ty = target_xy\n            dist[ty][tx] = 0\n            q = collections.deque([(tx, ty)])\n            while q:\n                x, y = q.popleft()\n                nd = dist[y][x] + 1\n                cur_t = self.base[y][x]\n                for d, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                    if cur_t in ONEWAY_TILES and cur_t[-1] != d:\n                        continue\n                    px, py = x - dx, y - dy\n                    if _in_bounds(px, py) and dist[py][px] > nd:\n                        if self.base[py][px] == \"T_W\":\n                            continue\n                        dist[py][px] = nd\n                        q.append((px, py))\n            return dist\n\n        self.dist_k1 = bfs_dist(self.k1_pos)\n        self.dist_k2 = bfs_dist(self.k2_pos)\n        self.dist_v = bfs_dist(self.v_pos)\n        self.dist_plates = [bfs_dist(p) for p in self.plates]\n\n        # Static dead box cells (corners of indestructible walls, non-plate, non-switch, non-mech-adj)\n        self.dead_box_cells = set()\n        if self.plates:\n            mech_adj = set()\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    t = self.base[y][x]\n                    if t in MECH_TILES or t in (\"PLATE\", \"SWITCH\"):\n                        for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                            for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                                mech_adj.add((x + dx, y + dy))\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    if (x, y) in mech_adj or (x, y) in self.plates_set:\n                        continue\n                    if self.base[y][x] in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\", \"PLATE\", \"SWITCH\"):\n                        continue\n                    def hb(nx, ny, pd):\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            return True\n                        nt = self.base[ny][nx]\n                        if nt in (\"T_W\", \"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                            return True\n                        if nt in ONEWAY_TILES and nt[-1] != pd:\n                            return True\n                        return False\n                    if (hb(x, y - 1, \"U\") or hb(x, y + 1, \"D\")) and (hb(x - 1, y, \"L\") or hb(x + 1, y, \"R\")):\n                        self.dead_box_cells.add((x, y))\n\n        # Static throw spots hitting SWITCH or MIMIC (including via MIRROR/ICE/PORTAL)\n        self.throw_candidates = collections.defaultdict(list)\n        if self.switches or self.mimics:\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    t = self.base[y][x]\n                    if t in (\"T_W\", \"W\", \"PIT\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                        continue\n                    for d in DIR_LIST:\n                        if self._static_throw_hits(x, y, d):\n                            self.throw_candidates[(x, y)].append(d)\n\n        # Static bomb spots: adjacent (3x3) to a destructible tile\n        self.bomb_candidates = set()\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                t = self.base[y][x]\n                if t in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                    continue\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    found = False\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n                        if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and self.base[ny][nx] in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY:\n                            found = True\n                            break\n                    if found:\n                        self.bomb_candidates.add((x, y))\n                        break\n\n    def _static_throw_hits(self, sx, sy, d):\n        cd = d\n        x, y = sx, sy\n        rng = 6\n        sp = 0\n        while rng > 0:\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n            x, y = x + dx, y + dy\n            rng -= 1\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                return False\n            t = self.base[y][x]\n            if t in (\"T_W\", \"W\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                return False\n            if t in (\"SWITCH\", \"MIMIC\"):\n                return True\n            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n            elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                if pp:\n                    x, y = pp\n                    sp += 1\n                    if sp == 2:\n                        return False\n        t6 = self.base[y][x]\n        if t6 == \"ICE\" or t6 in MIRROR_MAP:\n            for _ in range(32):\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                x, y = x + dx, y + dy\n                if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                    return False\n                t = self.base[y][x]\n                if t in (\"T_W\", \"W\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                    return False\n                if t in (\"SWITCH\", \"MIMIC\"):\n                    return True\n                if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                elif t == \"ICE\":\n                    continue\n                elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                    if pp:\n                        x, y = pp\n                        sp += 1\n                        if sp == 2:\n                            return False\n                    else:\n                        return False\n                else:\n                    return False\n        return False\n\n    def tile_at(self, x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        t = self.base[y][x]\n        if t == \"CRACK\":\n            if destr_m & self.destr_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"EMPTY\"\n            if crack_m & self.crack_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"PIT\"\n            return \"CRACK\"\n        if t == \"PIT\":\n            if pit_m & self.pit_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"EMPTY\"\n            return \"PIT\"\n        bit = self.destr_bit.get((x, y))\n        if bit and (destr_m & bit):\n            return \"EMPTY\"\n        return t\n\n    def plates_active(self, boxes):\n        if not self.plates_tuple:\n            return False\n        for p in self.plates_tuple:\n            if p not in boxes:\n                return False\n        return True\n\n    def is_high_wall(self, x, y, gf, p_act, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n            return True\n        t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n        if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n            return True\n        if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"P_GATE\" and not p_act:\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def blocks_oneway(self, x, y, cur_dir):\n        t = self.base[y][x]\n        if t in ONEWAY_TILES:\n            return t[-1] != cur_dir\n        return False\n\n    def get_laser_info(self, gf, boxes, tnts, destr_m):\n        if not self.has_lasers:\n            return None\n        p_act = self.plates_active(boxes)\n        key = (gf, p_act, boxes, tnts, destr_m)\n        cached = self.laser_cache.get(key)\n        if cached is not None:\n            return cached\n\n        red_arr = [0] * GRID_SIZE\n        blue_arr = [0] * GRID_SIZE\n        red_tnts = set()\n        blue_tnts = set()\n        box_set = set(boxes)\n        tnt_set = set(tnts)\n        haz_indices = set()\n\n        for idx, (lx, ly, period, d, lbit) in enumerate(self.lasers):\n            if destr_m & lbit:\n                continue\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n            cbit = 1 << idx\n            arr = red_arr if period == 3 else blue_arr\n            tset = red_tnts if period == 3 else blue_tnts\n            for dist in range(1, 11):\n                rx, ry = lx + dist * dx, ly + dist * dy\n                if not _in_bounds(rx, ry):\n                    break\n                if self.is_high_wall(rx, ry, gf, p_act, 0, 0, destr_m):\n                    break\n                if (rx, ry) in box_set:\n                    break\n                if (rx, ry) in tnt_set:\n                    tset.add((rx, ry))\n                    break\n                ridx = ry * 40 + rx\n                arr[ridx] |= cbit\n                haz_indices.add(ridx)\n\n        res = (red_arr, blue_arr, red_tnts, blue_tnts, haz_indices)\n        if len(self.laser_cache) < 20000:\n            self.laser_cache[key] = res\n        return res\n\n    def step(self, st, action):\n        \"\"\"Exact v2.2 transition.\"\"\"\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        if turn > MAX_TURNS:\n            return None, \"DEAD\", 0\n\n        box_set = set(boxes)\n        tnt_set = set(tnts)\n        exposed = {(hx, hy)}\n        armed_tnts = set()\n        bombs_list = list(active_bombs)\n        dmg_taken = 0\n        status = \"RUNNING\"\n\n        def cur_p_act():\n            if not self.plates_tuple:\n                return False\n            for p in self.plates_tuple:\n                if p not in box_set:\n                    return False\n            return True\n\n        def cur_hw(x, y):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                return True\n            t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n            if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n                return True\n            if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n                return True\n            if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n                return True\n            if t == \"P_GATE\" and not cur_p_act():\n                return True\n            return False\n\n        def can_box(x, y, d):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y) or cur_hw(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, d):\n                return False\n            t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n            if t in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                return False\n            if (x, y) in box_set or (x, y) in tnt_set or (x, y) == (hx, hy):\n                return False\n            return True\n\n        def can_hero(x, y, d):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y) or cur_hw(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, d):\n                return False\n            if (x, y) in box_set or (x, y) in tnt_set:\n                return False\n            return True\n\n        def displace_box(x0, y0, x1, y1, cur_dir):\n            nonlocal gf, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m\n            steps = 0\n            p_cnt = 0\n            sup = False\n            while True:\n                steps += 1\n                if self.tile_at(x0, y0, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m) == \"CRACK\":\n                    crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(x0, y0)]\n                box_set.discard((x0, y0))\n                box_set.add((x1, y1))\n                t1 = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                    box_set.discard((x1, y1))\n                    if self.base[y1][x1] == \"PIT\":\n                        pit_m |= self.pit_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    else:\n                        destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    break\n                elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                    gf = not gf\n\n                if steps >= 32:\n                    break\n                if sup:\n                    sup = False\n                    if p_cnt >= 2:\n                        break\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n\n                tc = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if tc == \"ICE\":\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n                elif tc in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[tc][cur_dir]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n                elif tc in SPRING_TILES:\n                    cur_dir = tc[-1]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    blk = None\n                    for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                        pkx, pky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                        if not can_box(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                            blk = k\n                            if _in_bounds(pkx, pky) and (pkx, pky) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                                armed_tnts.add((pkx, pky))\n                            break\n                    if blk == 1:\n                        break\n                    lk = (blk - 1) if blk is not None else 3\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, x1 + lk * dx, y1 + lk * dy\n                    continue\n                elif tc in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[tc])\n                    if pp is None or pp in box_set or pp in tnt_set or pp == (hx, hy):\n                        break\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                    sup = True\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, pp[0], pp[1]\n                    continue\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        def displace_hero(x1, y1, cur_dir):\n            nonlocal hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, crack_m, destr_m, dmg_taken, status\n            x0, y0 = hx, hy\n            steps = 0\n            p_cnt = 0\n            sup = False\n            stop_m = False\n            while True:\n                steps += 1\n                if self.tile_at(x0, y0, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m) == \"CRACK\":\n                    crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(x0, y0)]\n                hx, hy = x1, y1\n                exposed.add((hx, hy))\n                t1 = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                    hp -= 100\n                    dmg_taken += 100\n                elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                    hp -= 45\n                    dmg_taken += 45\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    stop_m = True\n                elif t1 == \"HEAL\":\n                    hp = min(100, hp + 30)\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                    gf = not gf\n\n                if hp <= 0:\n                    status = \"DEAD\"\n                    return\n\n                if t1 == \"K1\":\n                    kmask |= 1\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"K2\":\n                    kmask |= 2\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"V\" and kmask == 3 and hp > 0:\n                    status = \"CLEARED\"\n                    return\n\n                if steps >= 32 or stop_m:\n                    break\n                if sup:\n                    sup = False\n                    if p_cnt >= 2:\n                        break\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n\n                tc = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if tc == \"ICE\":\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n                elif tc in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[tc][cur_dir]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n                elif tc in SPRING_TILES:\n                    cur_dir = tc[-1]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    blk = None\n                    for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                        pkx, pky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                        if not can_hero(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                            blk = k\n                            break\n                    if blk == 1:\n                        break\n                    lk = (blk - 1) if blk is not None else 3\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, x1 + lk * dx, y1 + lk * dy\n                    continue\n                elif tc in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[tc])\n                    if pp is None or pp in box_set or pp in tnt_set:\n                        break\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                    sup = True\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, pp[0], pp[1]\n                    continue\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        if action == \"WAIT\":\n            pass\n        elif action == \"BOMB\":\n            if bombs_left > 0 and not any(b[0] == hx and b[1] == hy for b in bombs_list):\n                bombs_left -= 1\n                bombs_list.append((hx, hy, 3))\n        elif action.startswith(\"THROW_\"):\n            if throws > 0:\n                throws -= 1\n                sx, sy = hx, hy\n                cd = action[-1]\n                rng = 6\n                sp = 0\n                shat = False\n                stop_p = False\n                while rng > 0:\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                    sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                    rng -= 1\n                    if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or cur_hw(sx, sy) or (sx, sy) in box_set:\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if (sx, sy) in tnt_set:\n                        armed_tnts.add((sx, sy))\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    t = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                    if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                        gf = not gf\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                        destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(sx, sy)]\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                    elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                        pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                        if pp is not None and pp not in box_set and pp not in tnt_set:\n                            sx, sy = pp\n                            sp += 1\n                            if sp == 2:\n                                stop_p = True\n                                break\n                if not shat and not stop_p and rng == 0:\n                    t6 = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                    if t6 == \"ICE\" or t6 in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        for _ in range(32):\n                            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                            sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                            if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or cur_hw(sx, sy) or (sx, sy) in box_set:\n                                break\n                            if (sx, sy) in tnt_set:\n                                armed_tnts.add((sx, sy))\n                                break\n                            t = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                            if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                                gf = not gf\n                                break\n                            if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                                destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(sx, sy)]\n                                break\n                            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                                cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                            elif t == \"ICE\":\n                                continue\n                            elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                                pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                                if pp is not None and pp not in box_set and pp not in tnt_set:\n                                    sx, sy = pp\n                                    sp += 1\n                                    if sp == 2:\n                                        break\n                                else:\n                                    break\n                            else:\n                                break\n        elif action.startswith(\"MOVE_\"):\n            d = action[-1]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n            p1x, p1y = hx + dx, hy + dy\n            if _in_bounds(p1x, p1y) and not cur_hw(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d):\n                if (p1x, p1y) in box_set:\n                    p2x, p2y = hx + 2 * dx, hy + 2 * dy\n                    if can_box(p2x, p2y, d):\n                        displace_box(p1x, p1y, p2x, p2y, d)\n                        if can_hero(p1x, p1y, d):\n                            displace_hero(p1x, p1y, d)\n                    elif _in_bounds(p2x, p2y) and (p2x, p2y) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(p2x, p2y, d):\n                        armed_tnts.add((p2x, p2y))\n                elif (p1x, p1y) in tnt_set:\n                    pass\n                else:\n                    displace_hero(p1x, p1y, d)\n        elif action.startswith(\"JUMP_\"):\n            if jumps > 0:\n                jumps -= 1\n                d = action[-1]\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n                p1x, p1y = hx + dx, hy + dy\n                p2x, p2y = hx + 2 * dx, hy + 2 * dy\n                p1_ok = _in_bounds(p1x, p1y) and not cur_hw(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d)\n                if p1_ok and can_hero(p2x, p2y, d):\n                    displace_hero(p2x, p2y, d)\n\n        if status != \"RUNNING\":\n            ns = (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n                  tuple(sorted(box_set)), tuple(sorted(tnt_set)),\n                  crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, tuple(bombs_list), turn + 1)\n            return ns, status, dmg_taken\n\n        def detonate(seeds):\n            nonlocal hp, dmg_taken, crack_m, destr_m, status\n            exploded = set()\n            q = collections.deque(seeds)\n            while q:\n                ex, ey = q.popleft()\n                if (ex, ey) in exploded:\n                    continue\n                exploded.add((ex, ey))\n                tnt_set.discard((ex, ey))\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = ex + dx, ey + dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            continue\n                        if (nx, ny) == (hx, hy):\n                            hp -= 60\n                            dmg_taken += 60\n                        box_set.discard((nx, ny))\n                        if (nx, ny) in tnt_set:\n                            tnt_set.discard((nx, ny))\n                            if (nx, ny) not in exploded:\n                                q.append((nx, ny))\n                        nt = self.tile_at(nx, ny, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                        if nt == \"CRACK\":\n                            crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(nx, ny)]\n                        elif nt in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY:\n                            destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(nx, ny)]\n            if hp <= 0:\n                status = \"DEAD\"\n\n        det_seeds = set(armed_tnts)\n        next_bombs = []\n        for bx, by, bt in bombs_list:\n            bt -= 1\n            if bt <= 0:\n                det_seeds.add((bx, by))\n            else:\n                next_bombs.append((bx, by, bt))\n        if det_seeds:\n            detonate(det_seeds)\n        if status != \"RUNNING\":\n            return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n\n        new_boxes = tuple(sorted(box_set))\n        new_tnts = tuple(sorted(tnt_set))\n\n        if self.has_lasers and (turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0):\n            red_arr, blue_arr, red_tnts, blue_tnts, _ = self.get_laser_info(gf, new_boxes, new_tnts, destr_m)\n            hit_mask = 0\n            ltnts = set()\n            if turn % 3 == 0:\n                ltnts |= red_tnts\n                for ex, ey in exposed:\n                    m = red_arr[ey * 40 + ex]\n                    if m:\n                        hit_mask |= m\n            if turn % 4 == 0:\n                ltnts |= blue_tnts\n                for ex, ey in exposed:\n                    m = blue_arr[ey * 40 + ex]\n                    if m:\n                        hit_mask |= m\n            if hit_mask:\n                hits = hit_mask.bit_count()\n                hp -= 50 * hits\n                dmg_taken += 50 * hits\n                if hp <= 0:\n                    return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n            if ltnts:\n                detonate(ltnts)\n                if status != \"RUNNING\":\n                    return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n                new_boxes = tuple(sorted(box_set))\n                new_tnts = tuple(sorted(tnt_set))\n\n        ns = (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n              new_boxes, new_tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, tuple(next_bombs), turn + 1)\n        return ns, \"RUNNING\", dmg_taken\n\n    def _is_box_deadlocked(self, boxes):\n        if not self.plates_tuple:\n            return False\n        alive = 0\n        for b in boxes:\n            if b in self.plates_set or b not in self.dead_box_cells:\n                alive += 1\n        return alive < len(self.plates_tuple)\n\n    def heuristic(self, st):\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        h = 0\n        unfilled = [i for i, p in enumerate(self.plates_tuple) if p not in boxes]\n        if unfilled:\n            free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if b not in self.plates_set]\n            if len(unfilled) > len(free_boxes):\n                return 99999\n            used = 0\n            nearest_box_d = 999\n            for pi in unfilled:\n                px, py = self.plates_tuple[pi]\n                dp = self.dist_plates[pi]\n                best_d = 999\n                best_bi = -1\n                for bi, (bx, by) in enumerate(free_boxes):\n                    if used & (1 << bi):\n                        continue\n                    d = dp[by][bx]\n                    if d >= 999:\n                        d = abs(bx - px) + abs(by - py)\n                    if d < best_d:\n                        best_d = d\n                        best_bi = bi\n                if best_bi >= 0:\n                    used |= (1 << best_bi)\n                    h += best_d * 2 + 8\n                    bx, by = free_boxes[best_bi]\n                    hd = abs(hx - bx) + abs(hy - by)\n                    if hd < nearest_box_d:\n                        nearest_box_d = hd\n            if nearest_box_d < 999:\n                h += nearest_box_d\n\n        k1x, k1y = self.k1_pos\n        k2x, k2y = self.k2_pos\n        vx, vy = self.v_pos\n        d_k1 = self.dist_k1[hy][hx]\n        if d_k1 >= 999:\n            d_k1 = abs(hx - k1x) + abs(hy - k1y)\n        d_k2 = self.dist_k2[hy][hx]\n        if d_k2 >= 999:\n            d_k2 = abs(hx - k2x) + abs(hy - k2y)\n        d_v = self.dist_v[hy][hx]\n        if d_v >= 999:\n            d_v = abs(hx - vx) + abs(hy - vy)\n        d12 = abs(k1x - k2x) + abs(k1y - k2y)\n        d1v = abs(k1x - vx) + abs(k1y - vy)\n        d2v = abs(k2x - vx) + abs(k2y - vy)\n\n        if kmask == 0:\n            h += min(d_k1 + d12 + d2v, d_k2 + d12 + d1v) + 25\n        elif kmask == 1:\n            h += d_k2 + d2v + 12\n        elif kmask == 2:\n            h += d_k1 + d1v + 12\n        else:\n            h += d_v\n        return h\n\n    def _build_flat_grid(self, gf, p_act, boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        \"\"\"Build 1040-cell flat terrain & high-wall lookup in ~0.04ms.\"\"\"\n        grid = self.base_flat[:]\n        for _, _, idx, bit in self.destr_cells:\n            if destr_m & bit:\n                grid[idx] = \"EMPTY\"\n        for _, _, idx, bit in self.crack_cells:\n            if (crack_m & bit) and grid[idx] == \"CRACK\":\n                grid[idx] = \"PIT\"\n        for _, _, idx, bit in self.pit_cells:\n            if pit_m & bit:\n                grid[idx] = \"EMPTY\"\n\n        hw = bytearray(GRID_SIZE)\n        occ = bytearray(GRID_SIZE)  # 1 = BOX, 2 = TNT\n        for idx, t in enumerate(grid):\n            if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n            elif t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n            elif t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n            elif t == \"P_GATE\" and not p_act:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n        for bx, by in boxes:\n            occ[by * 40 + bx] = 1\n        for tx, ty in tnts:\n            occ[ty * 40 + tx] = 2\n        return grid, hw, occ\n\n    def expand_macros(self, st, deadline):\n        (sx, sy, shp, skmask, sgf, sjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n         sboxes, stnts, scrack_m, spit_m, sdestr_m, sactive_bombs, sturn) = st\n\n        if sactive_bombs:\n            out = []\n            for act in (\"MOVE_R\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_L\", \"WAIT\", \"JUMP_R\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_L\"):\n                ns, status, dmg = self.step(st, act)\n                if status == \"CLEARED\":\n                    return [act], []\n                if status == \"RUNNING\":\n                    out.append((ns, [act], dmg))\n            return None, out\n\n        p_act = self.plates_active(sboxes)\n        grid, hw, occ = self._build_flat_grid(sgf, p_act, sboxes, stnts, scrack_m, spit_m, sdestr_m)\n\n        if self.has_lasers:\n            red_arr, blue_arr, red_tnts, blue_tnts, haz_indices = self.get_laser_info(sgf, sboxes, stnts, sdestr_m)\n            has_live_lasers = len(haz_indices) > 0\n        else:\n            red_arr = blue_arr = None\n            haz_indices = ()\n            has_live_lasers = False\n\n        # Phase-sensitive cells: cells IN haz_indices OR within dist 2 of haz_indices OR adjacent to MECH_TILES\n        phase_sens = bytearray(GRID_SIZE)\n        if has_live_lasers:\n            for hidx in haz_indices:\n                hx0 = hidx % 40\n                hy0 = hidx // 40\n                for dy in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):\n                    ny = hy0 + dy\n                    if 1 <= ny < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                        rem = 2 - abs(dy)\n                        for dx in range(-rem, rem + 1):\n                            nx = hx0 + dx\n                            if 1 <= nx < GRID_WIDTH - 1:\n                                phase_sens[ny * 40 + nx] = 1\n            for idx, t in enumerate(grid):\n                if t in MECH_TILES:\n                    phase_sens[idx] = 1\n                    mx, my = idx % 40, idx // 40\n                    for _, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                        nx, ny = mx + dx, my + dy\n                        if 1 <= nx < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= ny < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                            phase_sens[ny * 40 + nx] = 1\n            phase_sens[sy * 40 + sx] = 1\n\n        # Push spots indexed by flat idx\n        push_spots = {}\n        for (bx, by) in sboxes:\n            for d, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                px, py = bx - dx, by - dy\n                if 1 <= px < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= py < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                    pidx = py * 40 + px\n                    if pidx not in push_spots:\n                        push_spots[pidx] = []\n                    push_spots[pidx].append((bx, by, d))\n\n        # Dynamic TNT throw spots\n        tnt_throw_spots = {}\n        if sthrows > 0 and stnts:\n            opp_map = {\"U\": \"D\", \"D\": \"U\", \"L\": \"R\", \"R\": \"L\"}\n            for (tx, ty) in stnts:\n                for d, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                    od = opp_map[d]\n                    for dist in range(1, 7):\n                        rx, ry = tx + dist * dx, ty + dist * dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(rx, ry):\n                            break\n                        ridx = ry * 40 + rx\n                        if hw[ridx] or occ[ridx] == 1:\n                            break\n                        if ridx not in tnt_throw_spots:\n                            tnt_throw_spots[ridx] = []\n                        tnt_throw_spots[ridx].append(od)\n\n        sidx = sy * 40 + sx\n        # Micro-Dijkstra using parent links (NO tuple concatenation per step!)\n        # Node info: nodes[nid] = (parent_nid, act_str)\n        m_nodes = [(None, None)]\n        # Dominance table: key = (idx, tmod_key, ccm) -> best (dmg, steps, -jumps)\n        # Wait: if jumps differ, keep best per (idx, tmod_key, ccm, jumps)\n        best_m = {}\n        best_cell_dom = {}  # (idx, tmod_key, ccm) -> list of non-dominated (dmg, jumps, steps)\n        tkey0 = (sturn % 12) if (has_live_lasers and phase_sens[sidx]) else 0\n        best_m[(sidx, tkey0, scrack_m, sjumps)] = (0, 0)\n\n        # PQ entry: (prio, dmg, steps, idx, hp, jumps, ccm, turn, nid)\n        pq = [(0, 0, 0, sidx, shp, sjumps, scrack_m, sturn, 0)]\n        best_cell_arrival = {}   # idx -> (dmg, steps, hp, jumps, ccm, turn, nid)\n        best_push_arrival = {}   # (bx, by, d, tmod_if_haz) -> (hp, jumps, ccm, turn, nid, idx)\n        best_bomb_arrival = {}   # idx -> (dmg, steps, hp, jumps, ccm, turn, nid)\n        emitted_events = {}      # key -> (dmg, steps, ns, nid, last_act)\n\n        def trace_path(nid, extra=None):\n            acts = []\n            cur = nid\n            while cur != 0:\n                p, a = m_nodes[cur]\n                acts.append(a)\n                cur = p\n            acts.reverse()\n            if extra is not None:\n                acts.append(extra)\n            return acts\n\n        def try_push_micro(ndmg, nsteps, nidx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, parent_nid, act_str):\n            if nturn > MAX_TURNS + 1:\n                return\n            tk = (nturn % 12) if (has_live_lasers and phase_sens[nidx]) else 0\n            # Fast dominance check: if we already arrived at (nidx, tk, ncm) with >= nj and <= (ndmg, nsteps), skip!\n            mk = (nidx, tk, ncm, nj)\n            prev = best_m.get(mk)\n            if prev is not None and (ndmg, nsteps) >= prev:\n                return\n            # Also check if higher jump count already dominates\n            if nj < sjumps:\n                for hj in range(nj + 1, sjumps + 1):\n                    hpv = best_m.get((nidx, tk, ncm, hj))\n                    if hpv is not None and hpv[0] <= ndmg and hpv[1] <= nsteps:\n                        return\n            best_m[mk] = (ndmg, nsteps)\n            cnid = len(m_nodes)\n            m_nodes.append((parent_nid, act_str))\n            nprio = ndmg * 600 + nsteps * 10 + (sjumps - nj) * 4 + (1 if ncm != scrack_m else 0) * 3\n            heapq.heappush(pq, (nprio, ndmg, nsteps, nidx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cnid))\n\n        pops = 0\n        max_pops = 4500 if has_live_lasers else 2200\n\n        while pq and pops < max_pops:\n            prio, cdmg, csteps, cidx, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            pops += 1\n            if (pops & 255) == 0 and time.perf_counter() > deadline:\n                break\n            if cturn > MAX_TURNS:\n                continue\n\n            tk = (cturn % 12) if (has_live_lasers and phase_sens[cidx]) else 0\n            pv = best_m.get((cidx, tk, ccm, cjumps))\n            if pv is not None and (cdmg, csteps) > pv:\n                continue\n\n            cx = cidx % 40\n            cy = cidx // 40\n\n            # Record best arrival at cidx\n            pca = best_cell_arrival.get(cidx)\n            if pca is None or (cdmg, csteps) < (pca[0], pca[1]):\n                best_cell_arrival[cidx] = (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid)\n\n            # Push trigger\n            ps_list = push_spots.get(cidx)\n            if ps_list is not None:\n                for (bx, by, d) in ps_list:\n                    bidx = by * 40 + bx\n                    ptk = (cturn % 12) if (has_live_lasers and (phase_sens[cidx] or phase_sens[bidx])) else 0\n                    pk = (bx, by, d, ptk)\n                    if pk not in best_push_arrival:\n                        best_push_arrival[pk] = (chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid, cidx, d)\n\n            # Bomb trigger\n            if sbombs > 0 and (cx, cy) in self.bomb_candidates:\n                if cidx not in best_bomb_arrival:\n                    best_bomb_arrival[cidx] = (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid)\n\n            # Precheck lasers for cturn\n            t3 = has_live_lasers and (cturn % 3 == 0)\n            t4 = has_live_lasers and (cturn % 4 == 0)\n            c_lmask = 0\n            if t3:\n                c_lmask |= red_arr[cidx]\n            if t4:\n                c_lmask |= blue_arr[cidx]\n\n            # 1. WAIT (only at phase-sensitive cells when live lasers exist)\n            if has_live_lasers and phase_sens[cidx]:\n                wdmg = 50 * c_lmask.bit_count() if c_lmask else 0\n                if chp > wdmg:\n                    try_push_micro(cdmg + wdmg, csteps + 1, cidx, chp - wdmg, cjumps, ccm, cturn + 1, cnid, \"WAIT\")\n\n            cur_tile = grid[cidx]\n            cur_is_crack = (cur_tile == \"CRACK\") and not (ccm & self.crack_bit[(cx, cy)])\n\n            # 2. MOVE_* and JUMP_*\n            for d, dx, dy, didx in DIR_DELTAS:\n                nidx = cidx + didx\n                if not hw[nidx] and not occ[nidx]:\n                    nt = grid[nidx]\n                    # Check oneway\n                    if nt in ONEWAY_TILES and nt[-1] != d:\n                        pass\n                    else:\n                        # FAST PATH: simple walkable tile (no crack on current tile, no mech/event on next tile!)\n                        if (not cur_is_crack) and (nt in SIMPLE_WALK_TILES) and (not (t3 and red_tnts)) and (not (t4 and blue_tnts)):\n                            lmask = c_lmask\n                            if t3:\n                                lmask |= red_arr[nidx]\n                            if t4:\n                                lmask |= blue_arr[nidx]\n                            sdmg = 50 * lmask.bit_count() if lmask else 0\n                            if chp > sdmg:\n                                try_push_micro(cdmg + sdmg, csteps + 1, nidx, chp - sdmg, cjumps, ccm, cturn + 1, cnid, f\"MOVE_{d}\")\n                        else:\n                            # Exact step for special tiles (ICE/MIRROR/SPRING/PORTAL/CRACK/K1/K2/V/SWITCH/HEAL/MIMIC/PIT)\n                            if nt == \"PIT\" or (nt == \"CRACK\" and (ccm & self.crack_bit[(cx + dx, cy + dy)])):\n                                pass  # walking directly into PIT is lethal\n                            else:\n                                act = f\"MOVE_{d}\"\n                                cur_st = (cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                                          sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n                                ns, nstatus, sdmg = self.step(cur_st, act)\n                                if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                                    return trace_path(cnid, act), []\n                                if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                                    (nx, ny, nhp, nkm, ngf, nj, _, _, _, _, ncm, _, ndm, _, nturn) = ns\n                                    ndmg = cdmg + sdmg\n                                    nsteps = csteps + 1\n                                    if nkm != skmask or ngf != sgf or ndm != sdestr_m:\n                                        ek = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, ncm, ndm, nturn % 12 if has_live_lasers else 0)\n                                        pe = emitted_events.get(ek)\n                                        if pe is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < (pe[0], pe[1]):\n                                            emitted_events[ek] = (ndmg, nsteps, ns, cnid, act)\n                                    else:\n                                        try_push_micro(ndmg, nsteps, ny * 40 + nx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cnid, act)\n\n                # JUMP_*\n                if cjumps > 0:\n                    nx2 = cx + 2 * dx\n                    ny2 = cy + 2 * dy\n                    if 1 <= nx2 < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= ny2 < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                        nidx2 = nidx + didx\n                        if not hw[nidx] and not hw[nidx2] and not occ[nidx2]:\n                            nt1 = grid[nidx]\n                            nt2 = grid[nidx2]\n                            # Check jump useful: mid tile isn't plain safe empty\n                            if (nt1 != \"EMPTY\" or occ[nidx] or (has_live_lasers and (red_arr[nidx] or blue_arr[nidx]))):\n                                if not (nt1 in ONEWAY_TILES and nt1[-1] != d) and not (nt2 in ONEWAY_TILES and nt2[-1] != d):\n                                    if nt2 != \"PIT\" and not (nt2 == \"CRACK\" and (ccm & self.crack_bit[(nx2, ny2)])):\n                                        if (not cur_is_crack) and (nt2 in SIMPLE_WALK_TILES) and (not (t3 and red_tnts)) and (not (t4 and blue_tnts)):\n                                            lmask = c_lmask\n                                            if t3:\n                                                lmask |= red_arr[nidx2]\n                                            if t4:\n                                                lmask |= blue_arr[nidx2]\n                                            sdmg = 50 * lmask.bit_count() if lmask else 0\n                                            if chp > sdmg:\n                                                try_push_micro(cdmg + sdmg, csteps + 1, nidx2, chp - sdmg, cjumps - 1, ccm, cturn + 1, cnid, f\"JUMP_{d}\")\n                                        else:\n                                            act = f\"JUMP_{d}\"\n                                            cur_st = (cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                                                      sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n                                            ns, nstatus, sdmg = self.step(cur_st, act)\n                                            if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                                                return trace_path(cnid, act), []\n                                            if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                                                (nx, ny, nhp, nkm, ngf, nj, _, _, _, _, ncm, _, ndm, _, nturn) = ns\n                                                ndmg = cdmg + sdmg\n                                                nsteps = csteps + 1\n                                                if nkm != skmask or ngf != sgf or ndm != sdestr_m:\n                                                    ek = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, ncm, ndm, nturn % 12 if has_live_lasers else 0)\n                                                    pe = emitted_events.get(ek)\n                                                    if pe is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < (pe[0], pe[1]):\n                                                        emitted_events[ek] = (ndmg, nsteps, ns, cnid, act)\n                                                else:\n                                                    try_push_micro(ndmg, nsteps, ny * 40 + nx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cnid, act)\n\n        macros = []\n        # 1. Emitted events\n        for (dmg, steps, ns, cnid, last_act) in emitted_events.values():\n            macros.append((ns, trace_path(cnid, last_act), dmg))\n\n        # 2. return (keys_cnt, p_filled, 1 if self.plates_active(st[8]) else 0, -self.heuristic(st), st[2])\n\n        h0 = self.heuristic(init_state)\n        pq = [(weight * h0, 0, 0, init_state)]\n parents[cid] = (nid, act_seq)\n                nh = self.heuristic(ns)\n                heapq.heappush(pq, (ng + weight * nh, ng, cid, ns))\n\n        return None, reconstruct(best_partial_id), ## 2026-11-20T01:13:38.510000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=219 message=msg_1f495c548001xWY7nrw3dJjHFC

**Analyzing Progress**

Wow, I'm making incredible headway! I've already solved 6 out of the 16 cases in under 240ms. I'm especially proud of the performance on `BENCH_WEAK`, which was solved in only 77 steps and 137ms. The rest, including `OFFICIAL_01`, `OFFICIAL_02`, `ASTRA`, and `DEEPSEEK` are also solved and I'm pushing to optimize those as well.


**Inspecting Bottlenecks**

I've made progress on `HYDROGEN` and `QWEN`, but now I'm focused on the remaining six cases. I'm deep-diving into the reasons for their slower performance, particularly `BENCH_STRONG`. It seems I'm expanding far too many nodes—161 for `BENCH_STRONG` up to step 111 out of ~115, a significant bottleneck. I suspect the high number of push macros and Micro-BFS's `max_pops` limit are key contributors to this inefficiency, which is also seen in `argon`, `carbon`, `glm`, `hunyuan`, and `sol`.


**Investigating a Case**

I'm now zeroing in on why `P1:sol` only got a partial solution, specifically the pathing around the portals. The map layout and portal mechanics are complex: to reach `K1` via `PORTAL_1A`, one *must* jump right to then move left into the portal, which teleports to `(33,2)`. From there it is possible to step left to `K1`, or to die! So the jumps, which I will call `JUMP_R` are critical and that explains the partial solution.


**Uncovering an Oversight**

Okay, I've got a critical new angle on why the solver missed the bomb strategy in `P1:sol`. It turns out there's a missed optimization opportunity. The solver didn't consider bombing a `W` wall because of an issue with how it was calculating `best_cell_arrival` for adjacent cells to bombable walls. The `adj_unreach` check in `expand_macros` isn't correctly identifying valid bomb locations, so the solver did not see this as a valid option. I suspect this will cause further issues on other cases.

## 2026-11-20T01:14:01.054000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=237 message=msg_1f4961d56001MVL76Q7dHzFrv3

**Discovering Gold in Diagnostics**

I'm incredibly excited! The diagnostic output is a treasure trove. All six maps surprisingly share the exact same three minor bugs. The first, a significant issue, reveals `emitted_events` duplicated a single event 12 times in each map. This calls for some fixes.


**Pinpointing The Core Issue**

I've zeroed in on the critical bug. Turns out `tmod` and every `jumps` operation was the culprit. Specifically, the inclusion of `nturn % 12` in `ek = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, ncm, ndm, nturn % 12)` and `tmod = st[14] % 12` in `state_key(st)` even outside laser zones caused 12 copies for each macro node. This caused a massive node explosion—a 144x blowup! The fix is straightforward. I'll include `tmod` only when the hero is in a laser cell and set `tmod = 0` otherwise, resulting in a 12x-100x reduction!


**Analyzing Hunyuan's Behavior**

I'm deep in the weeds with `hunyuan`. That 57ms runtime puzzled me, but the map's 38 scattered `CRACK` tiles are the culprit. Micro-BFS keyed on the `crack_m` bitmask, resulting in exponential node growth. Different crack tile traversal orders caused an explosion. The fix? I'll remove `ccm` from the `best_m` key, only tracking it in the node state. This ensures each cell is visited at most once per `(tk, nj)` combination, thus avoiding the exponential explosion!


**Addressing Jump Optimizations**

I've got a couple of important observations to share. First, I'm examining `best_push_arrival`. I see an opportunity to eliminate "useless" pushes where a standard push is already known to be faster. A `JUMP_U` push on the box at `(3,9)` to `(3,8)` that lands at `(3,10)` makes sense, but the jumps burned is wasted when `j=3` is reachable. I also looked at the Sokoban Box-Chain. The `BOX(23,13)` to `PIT(25,13)` transfer on `BENCH_STRONG` is the problem.


**Unraveling Puzzle Dependencies**

I'm focused on the nuances of this latest puzzle iteration. Specifically, I'm examining how moves relate to each other. For `BENCH_STRONG`, the issue was the sequence of moves that ended up filling the `PIT(25,13)`, which then activated lasers in `V(36,13)`. This led to a large number of nodes. Additionally, I'm looking at `P1:argon`, where a convoluted series of throws and interactions are needed. I'm focusing on the behavior of `THROW_U`.


**Uncovering Solver Anomalies**

I've been investigating some inconsistencies. It appears the issue with `P1:argon` stems from a 12x duplication of key K2 nodes at (34,21), which clogged the priority queue. I've also examined `P1:sol`, and it's clear the solver needs to *keep* all three jumps to bomb a wall out of the spawn corridor. However, `emitted_events` created 25 duplicate K1/K2 nodes with j=2, which caused heuristic dropping that hurt the process.


**Refining Optimization Strategy**

I'm focused on the implications of fixing Bug #1 on `P1:sol`. A* is now much more efficient: with only four `j=2` nodes, the `BOMB` node at (3,14) is expanded on the fifth node, massively improving the performance. This fix is critical. Regarding `P1:deepseek`, the 50 HP loss on Turn 3 is unavoidable. It seems a `THROW_R` is the key to managing the `deepseek` scenario.

Push trigger\n            ps_list = push_spots.get(cidx)\n            if ps_list is not None:\n                for (bx, by, d) in ps_list:\n                    bidx = by * 40 + bx\n                    ptk = (cturn % 12) if (has_live_lasers and (phase_sens[cidx] or phase_sens[bidx])) else 0\n                    pk = (bx, by, d, ptk)\n                    if pk not in best_push_arrival:\n                        best_push_arrival[pk] = (chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid, cidx, d)\n\n            # Bomb trigger\n            if sbombs > 0 and (cx, cy) in self.bomb_candidates:\n                if cidx not in best_bomb_arrival:\n                    best_bomb_arrival[cidx] = (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid)"

## 2026-11-20T01:16:04.657000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=289 message=msg_1f498002b001tSthqY2M26DfbZ

**Discovering Core Issues**

I've just uncovered not one, but three critical bugs simultaneously! The primary issue stems from an oversight in the bomb generation within `SOL` – specifically, `BOMB@(3,14)` wasn't created initially. Upon inspecting `adj_unreach`, I see the `JUMP_R` action in `SOL` is the root cause.


**Analyzing Jump Efficiency**

I've made a breakthrough! Examining `best_cell_arrival`, I found that `(3,16)` was indeed reachable via `JUMP_R`, but the critical insight is that `adj_unreach` was **`False`** for `(3,15)`, which meant the bomb at `(3,14)` was never considered. The general rule now includes bombing `W` walls not only when the other side is entirely unreachable, but also when it's only accessible by expending jumps. Using `adj_unreach_no_jump`, the bomb's value immediately increased to +35, solving `SOL` in mere milliseconds! I'm now looking at `BENCH_STRONG`.


**Identifying Jump Depletion**

I've hit paydirt! I just realized both `CARBON` and `BENCH_STRONG` are failing because they depleted all jumps early on. The solver used all 3 jumps to save a single move walking around boxes. This leaves it stranded with `j=0` when a jump is crucial, like the final pit in `BENCH_STRONG` or the vault in `CARBON`. I need to revisit the jump optimization logic.


**Revising Jump Penalty**

I've pinpointed the root cause of the jump depletion issue. It boils down to `try_push_micro` and Macro-A*'s `g`-cost functions. My calculations are off. Spending one `JUMP` was too cheap, incentivizing wasteful usage. A `JUMP` now has a much steeper penalty (at least 25 in `nprio` and 6 in g-cost), and I will enforce the jump penalty with new logic. I also realized Macro-A\* and `best_m`'s comparison methods need updating.


**Optimizing State Filtering**

I've made further refinements to the state filtering process. Specifically, I've adjusted the penalties in `try_push_micro`, imposing a significant jump penalty of `(sjumps - nj) * 45` to prioritize non-jump paths. Simultaneously, in `_run_macro_astar`, I've introduced penalties based on preserved resources, such as jumps (`(st[5] - ns[5]) * 18`), bombs (`(st[7] - ns[7]) * 12`), and throws (`(st[6] - ns[6]) * 4`). This ensures A\* favors resource conservation. I'm also examining the micro-BFS performance, especially concerning lasers.

## 2026-11-20T01:17:46.631000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=331 message=msg_1f4998e81001z4A4gd3tOXTWeO

**Analyzing Performance Breakthroughs**

I'm incredibly excited to report significant progress! Recently, I successfully tackled V22:BENCH_STRONG in a lightning-fast 287.7ms, achieving 100 HP across 27 nodes and 123 steps! Furthermore, V22:BENCH_WEAK was solved even quicker at 133.0ms with 5 nodes and 80 steps, also scoring 100 HP! Initial results show P1:argon to be similarly promising.


**Achieving Final Solutions**

I'm making incredible headway! I've now completed P1:glm in 111.5ms with a perfect 100 HP, 9 nodes, and 78 steps! Moreover, I crushed P1:hydrogen in 73.1ms and P1:qwen in 107.0ms, also achieving 100 HP. I solved P1:hunyuan in 620.1ms with 15 nodes, all with a score of 100 HP! My sights are set on achieving 100% completion by solving the final two challenges and then running stress tests on custom nightmare maps! I'm now looking at P1:carbon.


**Investigating Problem Solving**

I've hit a fascinating roadblock. To solve P1:carbon, I see I *must* grab K1 and K2 *before* hitting a switch and entering V. My solver didn't do this, however, and I need to figure out why. It seems the ice formation at K1 is the key. Specifically, I'm now focused on how the solver *gets* to K1 to begin with.

The lower `nj` variants are utterly useless without a strict improvement in damage or speed, so I need to filter these out ruthlessly to avoid this replication and subsequent explosion of states.

I suspect `CARBON` will perform in a similar fashion with the recent improvements.

I am really seeing how quickly the inefficiencies accrue in this process.

I am now implementing pruning strategies. I am prioritizing Pareto-optimal entries by grouping `emitted_events` and keeping only the best. Now, I will implement smart candidate selections for bombs, focusing on `j=3` spawn-break bombs first. I need to make sure I am accounting for two-turn safe escapes.

**Analyzing Remaining Problem**

I've made incredible progress! I've solved every single map except `P1:sol`, and most of them in a flash, between 23 and 280 milliseconds at 100 HP! I'm specifically looking at the `P1:carbon` and `P1:argon` maps, which were passed with 156 and 100 steps respectively, at 100 HP!


**Investigating Commonality and Sol**

I've made headway, finding a common problem. I see that `P1:sol` and `P1:carbon` both involve bomb spamming of `W` walls. `P1:sol` is stuck at `j=2`, expanding a huge number of bomb states even though the target is unreachable at that jump value. It also checks a static minimum-jump value upfront, which may or may not be the cause. I'm focusing my thoughts on this now. I've passed maps like `P1:hunyuan` and `V22:BENCH_STRONG` quickly as a baseline for understanding the issues.


**Optimizing Pruning Strategy**

I've got a fantastic new insight! I've realized I can run a backward 0-1 BFS from the goal, V, disregarding Pits when no boxes are available. If no boxes are available, the resulting `min_jumps_to_V` is a mathematically exact lower bound on jumps to reach the goal! For `P1:sol`, it's 3, meaning any state with fewer jumps is instantly deadlocked! This applies even with boxes, using `max(0, min_jumps_to_V - len(boxes))`. This is a huge optimization, potentially pruning branches immediately instead of after hundreds of nodes, particularly in dungeons with pit gauntlets like `BENCH_STRONG`.


**Refining Heuristic Calculations**

I've been analyzing `P1:carbon`, finding a subtle heuristic issue. Specifically, the heuristic unfairly favors bomb spamming `W` walls over walking to a switch that opens a gate blocking the goal. The switch is unfairly penalized in the A\* calculation. I need to ensure the switch expands before `BOMB`, correcting the weighted A\* bias and considering the scarcity of bombs.

## 2026-11-20T01:20:17.824000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e0b7605dbffedZYz5Lo6geIMxN seq=407 message=msg_1f49bdd19001DypjSH3UxM1bP6

**Celebrating Complete Victory!**

I'm ecstatic! All 16 problems are solved, and lightning fast, too – between 26 and 362 milliseconds each! The scoreboard showcases impressive performance, with consistent PASS results across all challenges and very quick times. It's a clean sweep, every test case nailed!


**Analyzing Performance Metrics**

I'm now diving deep into the performance metrics for a specific set of test cases. Each case shows a "PASS" status. I'm noting the steps, hit points, node counts, and most importantly, the execution times, which vary from a speedy 26.6 milliseconds to a slightly slower 362.9 milliseconds. My focus is on understanding these variations to pinpoint areas for potential optimization.


**Identifying Vulnerabilities in Dungeons**

I'm now focusing on potential weaknesses in the current solver against brutal v2.2 opponent dungeons. I've brainstormed six adversarial failure modes that could break it, like tightly coupled Sokoban puzzles with boxes on ice, or long straight-line pushes. This is to harden the solver against such scenarios and further improve its resilience.


**Refining Box Movement Heuristics**

I'm working on refining the heuristics to handle scenarios with multiple boxes in a single room. My current approach involves treating each 1-tile push as a macro node, and there are potentially hundreds of nodes. To improve, I'm implementing two upgrades: an exact minimum-weight bipartite matching algorithm for box-to-plate assignments and a more accurate single-box push distance table, accounting for wall constraints and special tiles. This should significantly enhance the solver's efficiency in these complex situations.


**Optimizing Distance Calculations**

I'm now making refinements to my distance calculations to improve accuracy and efficiency. Specifically, I'm working on a reverse-pull Box BFS for each plate, which takes less than 2 milliseconds! This aims to identify potential wall deadlocks more effectively. Additionally, I'm developing a 'Push-Off-Plate Penalty' system to avoid wasting resources on unnecessary moves. The goal is to avoid any false pruning, even when wall bombs are an option.


**Testing Multi-Box Efficiency**

I'm now focused on evaluating multi-box efficiency. With the refinements, a complex 4-plate/6-box Sokoban room solves in under 40 nodes, taking under 300ms. I'm excited to test the limits of this on a brutal synthetic 4-plate/5-box dungeon featuring Ice-Stoppers to verify the performance.



**Implementing Complex Explosions**

I've made a breakthrough! My code now correctly handles bounces off mirrors and through portals to hit TNT! I've updated the `_static_throw_hits` function to include this, which takes under 1ms. Now, arbitrary bank shots are correctly handled, covering TNT, switches, and even Mimics!

**Refining Bomb Escape Strategies**

I am now addressing potential failure modes involving bombs near boxes or plates blocked by one-way paths or walls. The existing `_find_fast_bomb_escape` function was too limited. To fix this, I have expanded it. I'm implementing a fallback 2-step escape search that handles escapes via ice, portals, and springs. This ensures no bomb-placement opportunities are missed, even if taking a single laser hit is required!


**Evaluating Laser Shielding**

I'm now confirming the solver's capabilities in pushing boxes to shield the hero from lasers. It appears my current implementation already supports this, as any reachable box push is emitted as a macro, and laser beams are immediately updated with the new box positions. The `_is_box_deadlocked` check seems solid. I will now exclude initial laser beam cells from `dead_box_cells` for maximum completeness.


**Prioritizing Solution Optimization**

I'm now implementing an Anytime Refinement Pass to boost both the score and reliability! I noticed there's unused Turn-1 budget even with a fast initial solve. If the first pass finds a quick solution, with good health or shorter plan, I'll run a second, more focused search. This aims to find an even better plan by pruning nodes using the best plan cost. A refined plan will be chosen by the `Score_Hero` metric. If the first pass fails, subsequent greedy searches will execute.


**Expanding Strategic Depth**

I'm now exploring advanced tactics involving complex sequences. I'm focusing on scenarios with multiple gates, particularly when `plates_active == True`. I've made key updates: I'm implementing a multi-turn approach, continuing the search from the saved heap if the first turn is unsuccessful, offering up to 2.5 seconds of total search time without risk of a timeout.

I'm also tackling failure mode 6, which is bombing a box on a plate to close a gate, then throwing through the resulting portal. I will remove the wall blocking during precomputation for the throw candidate and filter block shots in O(1) by allowing the dynamic step to happen.


**Analyzing Strategic Trade-offs**

I'm now digging into a specific map, examining a discrepancy in steps taken. My initial solve chose a longer path, and I'm investigating why, considering a more efficient bomb strategy. Analyzing cost functions reveals how the solver prioritizes local decisions, with a second pass potentially leading to a much shorter plan via bomb use. I'm focusing on why a longer path was initially selected, expecting the second pass will yield a more efficient solution.


**Tackling Brutal Dungeons**

I am now focusing on several "NIGHTMARE" challenges to test the solver's resilience. Nightmare_2 features multi-room Sokoban with dual lasers, Nightmare_3 requires complex bank shots through portals, and Nightmare_4 includes unavoidable mimic damage and a laser gauntlet with a heal. These are designed to push the solver and expose weaknesses. Nightmare_5 forces a box-as-shield strategy, while Nightmare_6 presents a long, tight-turn labyrinth.
