MECH_TILES or t in (\"PLATE\", \"SWITCH\"):\n                        for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                            for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                                safe_special.add((x + dx, y + dy))\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    if (x, y) in safe_special:\n                        continue\n                    if self.base[y][x] in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\", \"PLATE\", \"SWITCH\"):\n                        continue\n                    def hb(nx, ny, pd):\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            return True\n                        nt = self.base[ny][nx]\n                        if nt in (\"T_W\", \"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                            return True\n                        if nt in ONEWAY_TILES and nt[-1] != pd:\n                            return True\n                        return False\n                    if (hb(x, y - 1, \"U\") or hb(x, y + 1, \"D\")) and (hb(x - 1, y, \"L\") or hb(x + 1, y, \"R\")):\n                        self.dead_box_cells.add((x, y))\n\n        # Static throw spots hitting SWITCH, MIMIC, or initial TNT (including bank shots via MIRROR/ICE/PORTAL, ignoring destructible W!)\n        self.throw_candidates = collections.defaultdict(list)\n        if self.switches or self.mimics or self.init_tnt_set:\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    t = self.base[y][x]\n                    if t in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\"):\n                        continue\n                    for d in DIR_LIST:\n                        if self._static_throw_hits(x, y, d):\n                            self.throw_candidates[(x, y)].append(d)\n\n        # Static bomb spots: adjacent (3x3) to any destructible tile or initial TNT or PLATE\n        self.bomb_candidates = set()\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                t = self.base[y][x]\n                if t in (\"T_W\", \"PIT\") or t in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                    continue\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    found = False\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n                        if _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            nt = self.base[ny][nx]\n                            if nt in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY or (nx, ny) in self.init_tnt_set or nt == \"PLATE\":\n                                found = True\n                                break\n                    if found:\n                        self.bomb_candidates.add((x, y))\n                        break\n\n    def _static_throw_hits(self, sx, sy, d):\n        cd = d\n        x, y = sx, sy\n        rng = 6\n        sp = 0\n        while rng > 0:\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n            x, y = x + dx, y + dy\n            rng -= 1\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                return False\n            t = self.base[y][x]\n            if t == \"T_W\":\n                return False\n            if t in (\"SWITCH\", \"MIMIC\") or (x, y) in self.init_tnt_set:\n                return True\n            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n            elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                if pp:\n                    x, y = pp\n                    sp += 1\n                    if sp == 2:\n                        return False\n        t6 = self.base[y][x]\n        if t6 == \"ICE\" or t6 in MIRROR_MAP:\n            for _ in range(32):\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                x, y = x + dx, y + dy\n                if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                    return False\n                t = self.base[y][x]\n                if t == \"T_W\":\n                    return False\n                if t in (\"SWITCH\", \"MIMIC\") or (x, y) in self.init_tnt_set:\n                    return True\n                if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                elif t == \"ICE\":\n                    continue\n                elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                    if pp:\n                        x, y = pp\n                        sp += 1\n                        if sp == 2:\n                            return False\n                    else:\n                        return False\n                else:\n                    return False\n        return False\n\n    def tile_at(self, x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        t = self.base[y][x]\n        if t == \"CRACK\":\n            if destr_m & self.destr_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"EMPTY\"\n            if crack_m & self.crack_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"PIT\"\n            return \"CRACK\"\n        if t == \"PIT\":\n            if pit_m & self.pit_bit[(x, y)]:\n                return \"EMPTY\"\n            return \"PIT\"\n        bit = self.destr_bit.get((x, y))\n        if bit and (destr_m & bit):\n            return \"EMPTY\"\n        return t\n\n    def plates_active(self, boxes):\n        if not self.plates_tuple:\n            return False\n        for p in self.plates_tuple:\n            if p not in boxes:\n                return False\n        return True\n\n    def is_high_wall(self, x, y, gf, p_act, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n            return True\n        t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n        if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n            return True\n        if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"P_GATE\" and not p_act:\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def blocks_oneway(self, x, y, cur_dir):\n        t = self.base[y][x]\n        if t in ONEWAY_TILES:\n            return t[-1] != cur_dir\n        return False\n\n    def get_laser_info(self, gf, boxes, tnts, destr_m):\n        if not self.has_lasers:\n            return None\n        p_act = self.plates_active(boxes)\n        key = (gf, p_act, boxes, tnts, destr_m)\n        cached = self.laser_cache.get(key)\n        if cached is not None:\n            return cached\n\n        red_arr = [0] * GRID_SIZE\n        blue_arr = [0] * GRID_SIZE\n        red_tnts = set()\n        blue_tnts = set()\n        box_set = set(boxes)\n        tnt_set = set(tnts)\n        haz_indices = set()\n\n        for idx, (lx, ly, period, d, lbit) in enumerate(self.lasers):\n            if destr_m & lbit:\n                continue\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n            cbit = 1 << idx\n            arr = red_arr if period == 3 else blue_arr\n            tset = red_tnts if period == 3 else blue_tnts\n            for dist in range(1, 11):\n                rx, ry = lx + dist * dx, ly + dist * dy\n                if not _in_bounds(rx, ry):\n                    break\n                if self.is_high_wall(rx, ry, gf, p_act, 0, 0, destr_m):\n                    break\n                if (rx, ry) in box_set:\n                    break\n                if (rx, ry) in tnt_set:\n                    tset.add((rx, ry))\n                    break\n                ridx = ry * 40 + rx\n                arr[ridx] |= cbit\n                haz_indices.add(ridx)\n\n        res = (red_arr, blue_arr, red_tnts, blue_tnts, haz_indices)\n        if len(self.laser_cache) < 25000:\n            self.laser_cache[key] = res\n        return res\n\n    def step(self, st, action):\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        if turn > MAX_TURNS:\n            return None, \"DEAD\", 0\n\n        box_set = set(boxes)\n        tnt_set = set(tnts)\n        exposed = {(hx, hy)}\n        armed_tnts = set()\n        bombs_list = list(active_bombs)\n        dmg_taken = 0\n        status = \"RUNNING\"\n\n        def cur_p_act():\n            if not self.plates_tuple:\n                return False\n            for p in self.plates_tuple:\n                if p not in box_set:\n                    return False\n            return True\n\n        def cur_hw(x, y):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n                return True\n            t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n            if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n                return True\n            if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n                return True\n            if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n                return True\n            if t == \"P_GATE\" and not cur_p_act():\n                return True\n            return False\n\n        def can_box(x, y, d):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y) or cur_hw(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, d):\n                return False\n            t = self.tile_at(x, y, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n            if t in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                return False\n            if (x, y) in box_set or (x, y) in tnt_set or (x, y) == (hx, hy):\n                return False\n            return True\n\n        def can_hero(x, y, d):\n            if not _in_bounds(x, y) or cur_hw(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, d):\n                return False\n            if (x, y) in box_set or (x, y) in tnt_set:\n                return False\n            return True\n\n        def displace_box(x0, y0, x1, y1, cur_dir):\n            nonlocal gf, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m\n            steps = 0\n            p_cnt = 0\n            sup = False\n            while True:\n                steps += 1\n                if self.tile_at(x0, y0, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m) == \"CRACK\":\n                    crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(x0, y0)]\n                box_set.discard((x0, y0))\n                box_set.add((x1, y1))\n                t1 = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                    box_set.discard((x1, y1))\n                    if self.base[y1][x1] == \"PIT\":\n                        pit_m |= self.pit_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    else:\n                        destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    break\n                elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                    gf = not gf\n\n                if steps >= 32:\n                    break\n                if sup:\n                    sup = False\n                    if p_cnt >= 2:\n                        break\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n\n                tc = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if tc == \"ICE\":\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n                elif tc in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[tc][cur_dir]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_box(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and (nx, ny) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        armed_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                    break\n                elif tc in SPRING_TILES:\n                    cur_dir = tc[-1]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    blk = None\n                    for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                        pkx, pky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                        if not can_box(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                            blk = k\n                            if _in_bounds(pkx, pky) and (pkx, pky) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                                armed_tnts.add((pkx, pky))\n                            break\n                    if blk == 1:\n                        break\n                    lk = (blk - 1) if blk is not None else 3\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, x1 + lk * dx, y1 + lk * dy\n                    continue\n                elif tc in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[tc])\n                    if pp is None or pp in box_set or pp in tnt_set or pp == (hx, hy):\n                        break\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                    sup = True\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, pp[0], pp[1]\n                    continue\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        def displace_hero(x1, y1, cur_dir):\n            nonlocal hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, crack_m, destr_m, dmg_taken, status\n            x0, y0 = hx, hy\n            steps = 0\n            p_cnt = 0\n            sup = False\n            stop_m = False\n            while True:\n                steps += 1\n                if self.tile_at(x0, y0, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m) == \"CRACK\":\n                    crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(x0, y0)]\n                hx, hy = x1, y1\n                exposed.add((hx, hy))\n                t1 = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                    hp -= 100\n                    dmg_taken += 100\n                elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                    hp -= 45\n                    dmg_taken += 45\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                    stop_m = True\n                elif t1 == \"HEAL\":\n                    hp = min(100, hp + 30)\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                    gf = not gf\n\n                if hp <= 0:\n                    status = \"DEAD\"\n                    return\n\n                if t1 == \"K1\":\n                    kmask |= 1\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"K2\":\n                    kmask |= 2\n                    destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(x1, y1)]\n                elif t1 == \"V\" and kmask == 3 and hp > 0:\n                    status = \"CLEARED\"\n                    return\n\n                if steps >= 32 or stop_m:\n                    break\n                if sup:\n                    sup = False\n                    if p_cnt >= 2:\n                        break\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n\n                tc = self.tile_at(x1, y1, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                if tc == \"ICE\":\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n                elif tc in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cur_dir = MIRROR_MAP[tc][cur_dir]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n                    if can_hero(nx, ny, cur_dir):\n                        x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, nx, ny\n                        continue\n                    break\n                elif tc in SPRING_TILES:\n                    cur_dir = tc[-1]\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cur_dir]\n                    blk = None\n                    for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                        pkx, pky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                        if not can_hero(pkx, pky, cur_dir):\n                            blk = k\n                            break\n                    if blk == 1:\n                        break\n                    lk = (blk - 1) if blk is not None else 3\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, x1 + lk * dx, y1 + lk * dy\n                    continue\n                elif tc in PORTAL_PAIRS:\n                    pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[tc])\n                    if pp is None or pp in box_set or pp in tnt_set:\n                        break\n                    p_cnt += 1\n                    sup = True\n                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = x1, y1, pp[0], pp[1]\n                    continue\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        if action == \"WAIT\":\n            pass\n        elif action == \"BOMB\":\n            if bombs_left > 0 and not any(b[0] == hx and b[1] == hy for b in bombs_list):\n                bombs_left -= 1\n                bombs_list.append((hx, hy, 3))\n        elif action.startswith(\"THROW_\"):\n            if throws > 0:\n                throws -= 1\n                sx, sy = hx, hy\n                cd = action[-1]\n                rng = 6\n                sp = 0\n                shat = False\n                stop_p = False\n                while rng > 0:\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                    sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                    rng -= 1\n                    if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or cur_hw(sx, sy) or (sx, sy) in box_set:\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if (sx, sy) in tnt_set:\n                        armed_tnts.add((sx, sy))\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    t = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                    if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                        gf = not gf\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                        destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(sx, sy)]\n                        shat = True\n                        break\n                    if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                    elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                        pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                        if pp is not None and pp not in box_set and pp not in tnt_set:\n                            sx, sy = pp\n                            sp += 1\n                            if sp == 2:\n                                stop_p = True\n                                break\n                if not shat and not stop_p and rng == 0:\n                    t6 = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                    if t6 == \"ICE\" or t6 in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        for _ in range(32):\n                            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                            sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                            if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or cur_hw(sx, sy) or (sx, sy) in box_set:\n                                break\n                            if (sx, sy) in tnt_set:\n                                armed_tnts.add((sx, sy))\n                                break\n                            t = self.tile_at(sx, sy, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                            if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                                gf = not gf\n                                break\n                            if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                                destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(sx, sy)]\n                                break\n                            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                                cd = MIRROR_MAP[t][cd]\n                            elif t == \"ICE\":\n                                continue\n                            elif t in PORTAL_PAIRS and sp < 2:\n                                pp = self.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIRS[t])\n                                if pp is not None and pp not in box_set and pp not in tnt_set:\n                                    sx, sy = pp\n                                    sp += 1\n                                    if sp == 2:\n                                        break\n                                else:\n                                    break\n                            else:\n                                break\n        elif action.startswith(\"MOVE_\"):\n            d = action[-1]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n            p1x, p1y = hx + dx, hy + dy\n            if _in_bounds(p1x, p1y) and not cur_hw(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d):\n                if (p1x, p1y) in box_set:\n                    p2x, p2y = hx + 2 * dx, hy + 2 * dy\n                    if can_box(p2x, p2y, d):\n                        displace_box(p1x, p1y, p2x, p2y, d)\n                        if can_hero(p1x, p1y, d):\n                            displace_hero(p1x, p1y, d)\n                    elif _in_bounds(p2x, p2y) and (p2x, p2y) in tnt_set and not self.blocks_oneway(p2x, p2y, d):\n                        armed_tnts.add((p2x, p2y))\n                elif (p1x, p1y) in tnt_set:\n                    pass\n                else:\n                    displace_hero(p1x, p1y, d)\n        elif action.startswith(\"JUMP_\"):\n            if jumps > 0:\n                jumps -= 1\n                d = action[-1]\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[d]\n                p1x, p1y = hx + dx, hy + dy\n                p2x, p2y = hx + 2 * dx, hy + 2 * dy\n                p1_ok = _in_bounds(p1x, p1y) and not cur_hw(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, d)\n                if p1_ok and can_hero(p2x, p2y, d):\n                    displace_hero(p2x, p2y, d)\n\n        if status != \"RUNNING\":\n            ns = (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n                  tuple(sorted(box_set)), tuple(sorted(tnt_set)),\n                  crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, tuple(bombs_list), turn + 1)\n            return ns, status, dmg_taken\n\n        def detonate(seeds):\n            nonlocal hp, dmg_taken, crack_m, destr_m, status\n            exploded = set()\n            q = collections.deque(seeds)\n            while q:\n                ex, ey = q.popleft()\n                if (ex, ey) in exploded:\n                    continue\n                exploded.add((ex, ey))\n                tnt_set.discard((ex, ey))\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = ex + dx, ey + dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            continue\n                        if (nx, ny) == (hx, hy):\n                            hp -= 60\n                            dmg_taken += 60\n                        box_set.discard((nx, ny))\n                        if (nx, ny) in tnt_set:\n                            tnt_set.discard((nx, ny))\n                            if (nx, ny) not in exploded:\n                                q.append((nx, ny))\n                        nt = self.tile_at(nx, ny, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m)\n                        if nt == \"CRACK\":\n                            crack_m |= self.crack_bit[(nx, ny)]\n                        elif nt in DESTROY_TO_EMPTY:\n                            destr_m |= self.destr_bit[(nx, ny)]\n            if hp <= 0:\n                status = \"DEAD\"\n\n        det_seeds = set(armed_tnts)\n        next_bombs = []\n        for bx, by, bt in bombs_list:\n            bt -= 1\n            if bt <= 0:\n                det_seeds.add((bx, by))\n            else:\n                next_bombs.append((bx, by, bt))\n        if det_seeds:\n            detonate(det_seeds)\n        if status != \"RUNNING\":\n            return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n\n        new_boxes = tuple(sorted(box_set))\n        new_tnts = tuple(sorted(tnt_set))\n\n        if self.has_lasers and (turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0):\n            red_arr, blue_arr, red_tnts, blue_tnts, _ = self.get_laser_info(gf, new_boxes, new_tnts, destr_m)\n            hit_mask = 0\n            ltnts = set()\n            if turn % 3 == 0:\n                ltnts |= red_tnts\n                for ex, ey in exposed:\n                    m = red_arr[ey * 40 + ex]\n                    if m:\n                        hit_mask |= m\n            if turn % 4 == 0:\n                ltnts |= blue_tnts\n                for ex, ey in exposed:\n                    m = blue_arr[ey * 40 + ex]\n                    if m:\n                        hit_mask |= m\n            if hit_mask:\n                hits = hit_mask.bit_count()\n                hp -= 50 * hits\n                dmg_taken += 50 * hits\n                if hp <= 0:\n                    return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n            if ltnts:\n                detonate(ltnts)\n                if status != \"RUNNING\":\n                    return None, \"DEAD\", dmg_taken\n                new_boxes = tuple(sorted(box_set))\n                new_tnts = tuple(sorted(tnt_set))\n\n        ns = (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n              new_boxes, new_tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, tuple(next_bombs), turn + 1)\n        return ns, \"RUNNING\", dmg_taken\n\n    def _is_box_deadlocked(self, boxes):\n        if not self.plates_tuple:\n            return False\n        alive = 0\n        for b in boxes:\n            if b in self.plates_set or b not in self.dead_box_cells:\n                alive += 1\n        return alive < len(self.plates_tuple)\n\n    def _min_plate_matching(self, unfilled_idxs, free_boxes):\n        \"\"\"Exact minimum-weight bipartite matching (<= 4 plates, <= 6 boxes).\"\"\"\n        nu = len(unfilled_idxs)\n        nb = len(free_boxes)\n        if nu == 0:\n            return 0, 0\n        if nb < nu:\n            return 99999, 0\n        # Precompute cost matrix\n        costs = []\n        for pi in unfilled_idxs:\n            px, py = self.plates_tuple[pi]\n            dp = self.dist_plates[pi]\n            row = []\n            for bx, by in free_boxes:\n                d = dp[by][bx]\n                if d >= 999:\n                    d = abs(bx - px) + abs(by - py)\n                row.append(d)\n            costs.append(row)\n\n        # Bitmask DP over free_boxes (size <= 64)\n        dp_m = {0: 0}\n        for i in range(nu):\n            ndp = {}\n            crow = costs[i]\n            for mask, val in dp_m.items():\n                for bi in range(nb):\n                    bit = 1 << bi\n                    if not (mask & bit):\n                        nmask = mask | bit\n                        nv = val + crow[bi]\n                        prev = ndp.get(nmask)\n                        if prev is None or nv < prev:\n                            ndp[nmask] = nv\n            dp_m = ndp\n        best_val = 99999\n        best_mask = 0\n        for mask, val in dp_m.items():\n            if val < best_val:\n                best_val = val\n                best_mask = mask\n        return best_val, best_mask\n\n    def heuristic(self, st, plates_only=False):\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        h = 0\n        unfilled = [i for i, p in enumerate(self.plates_tuple) if p not in boxes]\n        if unfilled:\n            free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if b not in self.plates_set]\n            mval, mmask = self._min_plate_matching(unfilled, free_boxes)\n            if mval >= 99999:\n                return 99999\n            h += mval * 2 + len(unfilled) * 8\n            nearest = 999\n            for bi, (bx, by) in enumerate(free_boxes):\n                if mmask & (1 << bi):\n                    hd = abs(hx - bx) + abs(hy - by)\n                    if hd < nearest:\n                        nearest = hd\n            if nearest < 999:\n                h += nearest\n        if plates_only:\n            return h\n\n        k1x, k1y = self.k1_pos\n        k2x, k2y = self.k2_pos\n        vx, vy = self.v_pos\n        d_k1 = self.dist_k1[hy][hx]\n        if d_k1 >= 999:\n            d_k1 = abs(hx - k1x) + abs(hy - k1y)\n        d_k2 = self.dist_k2[hy][hx]\n        if d_k2 >= 999:\n            d_k2 = abs(hx - k2x) + abs(hy - k2y)\n        d_v = self.dist_v[hy][hx]\n        if d_v >= 999:\n            d_v = abs(hx - vx) + abs(hy - vy)\n        d12 = abs(k1x - k2x) + abs(k1y - k2y)\n        d1v = abs(k1x - vx) + abs(k1y - vy)\n        d2v = abs(k2x - vx) + abs(k2y - vy)\n\n        if kmask == 0:\n            h += min(d_k1 + d12 + d2v, d_k2 + d12 + d1v) + 25\n        elif kmask == 1:\n            h += d_k2 + d2v + 12\n        elif kmask == 2:\n            h += d_k1 + d1v + 12\n        else:\n            h += d_v\n        return h\n\n    def _build_flat_grid(self, gf, p_act, boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m):\n        grid = self.base_flat[:]\n        for _, _, idx, bit in self.destr_cells:\n            if destr_m & bit:\n                grid[idx] = \"EMPTY\"\n        for _, _, idx, bit in self.crack_cells:\n            if (crack_m & bit) and grid[idx] == \"CRACK\":\n                grid[idx] = \"PIT\"\n        for _, _, idx, bit in self.pit_cells:\n            if pit_m & bit:\n                grid[idx] = \"EMPTY\"\n\n        hw = bytearray(GRID_SIZE)\n        occ = bytearray(GRID_SIZE)\n        for idx, t in enumerate(grid):\n            if t in HIGH_WALL_STATIC:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n            elif t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n            elif t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n            elif t == \"P_GATE\" and not p_act:\n                hw[idx] = 1\n        for bx, by in boxes:\n            occ[by * 40 + bx] = 1\n        for tx, ty in tnts:\n            occ[ty * 40 + tx] = 2\n        return grid, hw, occ\n\n    def expand_macros(self, st, deadline):\n        (sx, sy, shp, skmask, sgf, sjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n         sboxes, stnts, scrack_m, spit_m, sdestr_m, sactive_bombs, sturn) = st\n\n        if sactive_bombs:\n            out = []\n            for act in (\"MOVE_R\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_L\", \"WAIT\", \"JUMP_R\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_L\"):\n                ns, status, dmg = self.step(st, act)\n                if status == \"CLEARED\":\n                    return [act], []\n                if status == \"RUNNING\":\n                    out.append((ns, [act], dmg))\n            return None, out\n\n        p_act = self.plates_active(sboxes)\n        grid, hw, occ = self._build_flat_grid(sgf, p_act, sboxes, stnts, scrack_m, spit_m, sdestr_m)\n\n        if self.has_lasers:\n            red_arr, blue_arr, red_tnts, blue_tnts, haz_indices = self.get_laser_info(sgf, sboxes, stnts, sdestr_m)\n            has_live_lasers = len(haz_indices) > 0\n            if has_live_lasers:\n                has_r = any(red_arr[i] for i in haz_indices)\n                has_b = any(blue_arr[i] for i in haz_indices)\n                lperiod = 12 if (has_r and has_b) else (3 if has_r else 4)\n            else:\n                lperiod = 1\n        else:\n            red_arr = blue_arr = None\n            haz_indices = ()\n            has_live_lasers = False\n            lperiod = 1\n\n        phase_sens = bytearray(GRID_SIZE)\n        if has_live_lasers:\n            for hidx in haz_indices:\n                phase_sens[hidx] = 1\n                hx0 = hidx % 40\n                hy0 = hidx // 40\n                for _, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                    nx, ny = hx0 + dx, hy0 + dy\n                    if 1 <= nx < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= ny < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                        phase_sens[ny * 40 + nx] = 1\n            for idx, t in enumerate(grid):\n                if t in MECH_TILES:\n                    phase_sens[idx] = 1\n                    mx, my = idx % 40, idx // 40\n                    for _, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                        nx, ny = mx + dx, my + dy\n                        if 1 <= nx < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= ny < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                            phase_sens[ny * 40 + nx] = 1\n            phase_sens[sy * 40 + sx] = 1\n\n        push_spots = {}\n        for (bx, by) in sboxes:\n            for d, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                px, py = bx - dx, by - dy\n                if 1 <= px < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= py < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                    pidx = py * 40 + px\n                    if pidx not in push_spots:\n                        push_spots[pidx] = []\n                    push_spots[pidx].append((bx, by, d))\n\n        tnt_throw_spots = {}\n        if sthrows > 0 and stnts:\n            opp_map = {\"U\": \"D\", \"D\": \"U\", \"L\": \"R\", \"R\": \"L\"}\n            for (tx, ty) in stnts:\n                for d, dx, dy, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                    od = opp_map[d]\n                    for dist in range(1, 7):\n                        rx, ry = tx + dist * dx, ty + dist * dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(rx, ry):\n                            break\n                        ridx = ry * 40 + rx\n                        if hw[ridx] or occ[ridx] == 1:\n                            break\n                        if ridx not in tnt_throw_spots:\n                            tnt_throw_spots[ridx] = []\n                        tnt_throw_spots[ridx].append(od)\n\n        sidx = sy * 40 + sx\n        m_nodes = [(None, None)]\n        best_m = {}\n        tkey0 = (sturn % lperiod) if (has_live_lasers and phase_sens[sidx]) else 0\n        best_m[(sidx, tkey0, sjumps)] = (0, 0)\n\n        pq = [(0, 0, 0, sidx, shp, sjumps, scrack_m, sturn, 0)]\n        best_cell_arrival = {}\n        reached_no_jump = set()\n        best_push_arrival = {}\n        best_bomb_arrival = {}\n        emitted_events = {}\n\n        def trace_path(nid, extra=None):\n            acts = []\n            cur = nid\n            while cur != 0:\n                p, a = m_nodes[cur]\n                acts.append(a)\n                cur = p\n            acts.reverse()\n            if extra is not None:\n                acts.append(extra)\n            return acts\n\n        def try_push_micro(ndmg, nsteps, nidx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, parent_nid, act_str):\n            if nturn > MAX_TURNS + 1:\n                return\n            tk = (nturn % lperiod) if (has_live_lasers and phase_sens[nidx]) else 0\n            mk = (nidx, tk, nj)\n            prev = best_m.get(mk)\n            if prev is not None and (ndmg, nsteps) >= prev:\n                return\n            if nj < sjumps:\n                for hj in range(nj + 1, sjumps + 1):\n                    hpv = best_m.get((nidx, tk, hj))\n                    if hpv is not None and hpv[0] <= ndmg and hpv[1] <= nsteps:\n                        return\n            best_m[mk] = (ndmg, nsteps)\n            cnid = len(m_nodes)\n            m_nodes.append((parent_nid, act_str))\n            nprio = ndmg * 600 + nsteps * 10 + (sjumps - nj) * 55 + (1 if ncm != scrack_m else 0) * 3\n            heapq.heappush(pq, (nprio, ndmg, nsteps, nidx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cnid))\n\n        pops = 0\n        max_pops = 4200 if lperiod == 12 else (2800 if has_live_lasers else 2000)\n\n        while pq and pops < max_pops:\n            prio, cdmg, csteps, cidx, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            pops += 1\n            if (pops & 255) == 0 and time.perf_counter() > deadline:\n                break\n            if cturn > MAX_TURNS:\n                continue\n\n            tk = (cturn % lperiod) if (has_live_lasers and phase_sens[cidx]) else 0\n            pv = best_m.get((cidx, tk, cjumps))\n            if pv is not None and (cdmg, csteps) > pv:\n                continue\n\n            cx = cidx % 40\n            cy = cidx // 40\n\n            if cjumps == sjumps and cdmg == 0:\n                reached_no_jump.add(cidx)\n\n            pca = best_cell_arrival.get(cidx)\n            if pca is None or (cdmg, sjumps - cjumps, csteps) < (pca[0], sjumps - pca[3], pca[1]):\n                best_cell_arrival[cidx] = (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid)\n\n            ps_list = push_spots.get(cidx)\n            if ps_list is not None:\n                for (bx, by, d) in ps_list:\n                    bidx = by * 40 + bx\n                    ptk = (cturn % lperiod) if (has_live_lasers and (cidx in haz_indices or bidx in haz_indices)) else 0\n                    pk = (bx, by, d, ptk)\n                    pp = best_push_arrival.get(pk)\n                    p_rank = (cdmg, sjumps - cjumps, csteps)\n                    if pp is None or p_rank < pp[0]:\n                        best_push_arrival[pk] = (p_rank, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid, cidx, d)\n\n            if sbombs > 0 and (cx, cy) in self.bomb_candidates:\n                pb = best_bomb_arrival.get(cidx)\n                if pb is None or (cdmg, sjumps - cjumps, csteps) < (pb[0], sjumps - pb[3], pb[1]):\n                    best_bomb_arrival[cidx] = (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid)\n\n            t3 = has_live_lasers and (cturn % 3 == 0)\n            t4 = has_live_lasers and (cturn % 4 == 0)\n            c_lmask = 0\n            if t3:\n                c_lmask |= red_arr[cidx]\n            if t4:\n                c_lmask |= blue_arr[cidx]\n\n            if has_live_lasers and phase_sens[cidx]:\n                wdmg = 50 * c_lmask.bit_count() if c_lmask else 0\n                if chp > wdmg:\n                    try_push_micro(cdmg + wdmg, csteps + 1, cidx, chp - wdmg, cjumps, ccm, cturn + 1, cnid, \"WAIT\")\n\n            cur_tile = grid[cidx]\n            cur_is_crack = (cur_tile == \"CRACK\") and not (ccm & self.crack_bit[(cx, cy)])\n\n            for d, dx, dy, didx in DIR_DELTAS:\n                nidx = cidx + didx\n                if not hw[nidx] and not occ[nidx]:\n                    nt = grid[nidx]\n                    if not (nt in ONEWAY_TILES and nt[-1] != d):\n                        if (not cur_is_crack) and (nt in SIMPLE_WALK_TILES) and (not (t3 and red_tnts)) and (not (t4 and blue_tnts)):\n                            lmask = c_lmask\n                            if t3:\n                                lmask |= red_arr[nidx]\n                            if t4:\n                                lmask |= blue_arr[nidx]\n                            sdmg = 50 * lmask.bit_count() if lmask else 0\n                            if chp > sdmg:\n                                try_push_micro(cdmg + sdmg, csteps + 1, nidx, chp - sdmg, cjumps, ccm, cturn + 1, cnid, f\"MOVE_{d}\")\n                        else:\n                            if nt != \"PIT\" and not (nt == \"CRACK\" and (ccm & self.crack_bit[(cx + dx, cy + dy)])):\n                                act = f\"MOVE_{d}\"\n                                cur_st = (cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                                          sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n                                ns, nstatus, sdmg = self.step(cur_st, act)\n                                if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                                    return trace_path(cnid, act), []\n                                if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                                    (nx, ny, nhp, nkm, ngf, nj, _, _, _, _, ncm, _, ndm, _, nturn) = ns\n                                    ndmg = cdmg + sdmg\n                                    nsteps = csteps + 1\n                                    if nkm != skmask or ngf != sgf or ndm != sdestr_m:\n                                        etk = (nturn % lperiod) if (has_live_lasers and (ny * 40 + nx) in haz_indices) else 0\n                                        ek = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, nj, ncm, ndm, etk)\n                                        pe = emitted_events.get(ek)\n                                        if pe is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < (pe[0], pe[1]):\n                                            emitted_events[ek] = (ndmg, nsteps, ns, cnid, act)\n                                    else:\n                                        try_push_micro(ndmg, nsteps, ny * 40 + nx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cnid, act)\n\n                if cjumps > 0:\n                    nx2 = cx + 2 * dx\n                    ny2 = cy + 2 * dy\n                    if 1 <= nx2 < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= ny2 < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                        nidx2 = nidx + didx\n                        if not hw[nidx] and not hw[nidx2] and not occ[nidx2]:\n                            nt1 = grid[nidx]\n                            nt2 = grid[nidx2]\n                            if (nt1 != \"EMPTY\" or occ[nidx] or (has_live_lasers and (red_arr[nidx] or blue_arr[nidx]))):\n                                if not (nt1 in ONEWAY_TILES and nt1[-1] != d) and not (nt2 in ONEWAY_TILES and nt2[-1] != d):\n                                    if nt2 != \"PIT\" and not (nt2 == \"CRACK\" and (ccm & self.crack_bit[(nx2, ny2)])):\n                                        if (not cur_is_crack) and (nt2 in SIMPLE_WALK_TILES) and (not (t3 and red_tnts)) and (not (t4 and blue_tnts)):\n                                            lmask = c_lmask\n                                            if t3:\n                                                lmask |= red_arr[nidx2]\n                                            if t4:\n                                                lmask |= blue_arr[nidx2]\n                                            sdmg = 50 * lmask.bit_count() if lmask else 0\n                                            if chp > sdmg:\n                                                try_push_micro(cdmg + sdmg, csteps + 1, nidx2, chp - sdmg, cjumps - 1, ccm, cturn + 1, cnid, f\"JUMP_{d}\")\n                                        else:\n                                            act = f\"JUMP_{d}\"\n                                            cur_st = (cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                                                      sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n                                            ns, nstatus, sdmg = self.step(cur_st, act)\n                                            if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                                                return trace_path(cnid, act), []\n                                            if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                                                (nx, ny, nhp, nkm, ngf, nj, _, _, _, _, ncm, _, ndm, _, nturn) = ns\n                                                ndmg = cdmg + sdmg\n                                                nsteps = csteps + 1\n                                                if nkm != skmask or ngf != sgf or ndm != sdestr_m:\n                                                    etk = (nturn % lperiod) if (has_live_lasers and (ny * 40 + nx) in haz_indices) else 0\n                                                    ek = (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, nj, ncm, ndm, etk)\n                                                    pe = emitted_events.get(ek)\n                                                    if pe is None or (ndmg, nsteps) < (pe[0], pe[1]):\n                                                        emitted_events[ek] = (ndmg, nsteps, ns, cnid, act)\n                                                else:\n                                                    try_push_micro(ndmg, nsteps, ny * 40 + nx, nhp, nj, ncm, nturn, cnid, act)\n\n        macros = []\n        # 1. Emitted events (Pareto-pruned on dmg, -jumps, steps)\n        ev_by_base = collections.defaultdict(list)\n        for (nx, ny, nkm, ngf, nj, ncm, ndm, etk), val in emitted_events.items():\n            ev_by_base[(nx, ny, nkm, ngf, ndm, etk)].append((val[0], -nj, val[1], val[2], val[3], val[4]))\n        for group in ev_by_base.values():\n            group.sort()\n            kept = []\n            for dmg, neg_j, steps, ns, cnid, last_act in group:\n                dom = False\n                for kdmg, kneg_j, ksteps in kept:\n                    if kdmg <= dmg and kneg_j <= neg_j and ksteps <= steps + 10:\n                        dom = True\n                        break\n                if not dom:\n                    kept.append((dmg, neg_j, steps))\n                    macros.append((ns, trace_path(cnid, last_act), dmg))\n\n        # 2. BOX pushes\n        for (_, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid, cidx, d) in best_push_arrival.values():\n            cx, cy = cidx % 40, cidx // 40\n            cur_st = (cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                      sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n            act = f\"MOVE_{d}\"\n            ns, nstatus, _ = self.step(cur_st, act)\n            if nstatus == \"CLEARED\":\n                return trace_path(cnid, act), []\n            if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                if ns[8] != sboxes or ns[9] != stnts or ns[4] != sgf or ns[11] != spit_m or ns[12] != sdestr_m:\n                    if not self._is_box_deadlocked(ns[8]):\n                        macros.append((ns, trace_path(cnid, act), max(0, shp - ns[2])))\n\n        # 3. Useful THROW transitions\n        if sthrows > 0:\n            seen_throw = set()\n            for cidx, (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid) in best_cell_arrival.items():\n                cx, cy = cidx % 40, cidx // 40\n                cdirs = self.throw_candidates.get((cx, cy))\n                tdirs = tnt_throw_spots.get(cidx)\n                if not cdirs and not tdirs:\n                    continue\n                dirs = set(cdirs) if cdirs else set()\n                if tdirs:\n                    dirs.update(tdirs)\n                cur_st = (cx, cy, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                          sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n                for d in dirs:\n                    act = f\"THROW_{d}\"\n                    ns, nstatus, _ = self.step(cur_st, act)\n                    if nstatus == \"RUNNING\":\n                        if ns[4] != sgf or ns[9] != stnts or ns[12] != sdestr_m:\n                            ek = (ns[4], ns[9], ns[12])\n                            if ek not in seen_throw:\n                                seen_throw.add(ek)\n                                macros.append((ns, trace_path(cnid, act), max(0, shp - ns[2])))\n\n        # 4. Useful BOMB + 2-turn safe escape\n        if sbombs > 0 and best_bomb_arrival:\n            by_effect = {}\n            for cidx, info in best_bomb_arrival.items():\n                bx, by = cidx % 40, cidx // 40\n                cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid = info\n                val = 0\n                hits = []\n                for dy in (-1, 0, 1):\n                    for dx in (-1, 0, 1):\n                        nx, ny = bx + dx, by + dy\n                        if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            continue\n                        nidx = ny * 40 + nx\n                        if occ[nidx] == 2:\n                            val += 60\n                            hits.append(nidx)\n                        elif occ[nidx] == 1 and (nx, ny) in self.plates_set:\n                            val += 25\n                            hits.append(nidx)\n                        nt = grid[nidx]\n                        if nt in ALL_LASER_TILES:\n                            val += 50\n                            hits.append(nidx)\n                        elif nt in (\"R_GATE\", \"B_GATE\"):\n                            val += 45\n                            hits.append(nidx)\n                        elif nt == \"MIMIC\":\n                            val += 30\n                            hits.append(nidx)\n                        elif nt in SPRING_TILES or nt in MIRROR_MAP:\n                            val += 15\n                            hits.append(nidx)\n                        elif nt == \"W\":\n                            adj_unreach = False\n                            for _, adx, ady, _ in DIR_DELTAS:\n                                ax, ay = nx + adx, ny + ady\n                                if 1 <= ax < GRID_WIDTH - 1 and 1 <= ay < GRID_HEIGHT - 1:\n                                    aidx = ay * 40 + ax\n                                    if aidx not in reached_no_jump and grid[aidx] != \"T_W\" and grid[aidx] != \"W\":\n                                        adj_unreach = True\n                                        break\n                            if adj_unreach:\n                                val += 35\n                                hits.append(nidx)\n                            elif (abs(nx - self.k1_pos[0]) + abs(ny - self.k1_pos[1]) <= 2\n                                  or abs(nx - self.k2_pos[0]) + abs(ny - self.k2_pos[1]) <= 2\n                                  or abs(nx - self.v_pos[0]) + abs(ny - self.v_pos[1]) <= 2):\n                                val += 40\n                                hits.append(nidx)\n                if val > 0 and hits:\n                    hk = tuple(hits)\n                    prev = by_effect.get(hk)\n                    rank_b = (-val, sjumps - cjumps, csteps)\n                    if prev is None or rank_b < prev[0]:\n                        by_effect[hk] = (rank_b, cidx, info)\n\n            cands = sorted(by_effect.values(), key=lambda z: z[0])[:10]\n            fallback_used = 0\n            for _, bidx, (cdmg, csteps, chp, cjumps, ccm, cturn, cnid) in cands:\n                if time.perf_counter() > deadline:\n                    break\n                bx, by = bidx % 40, bidx // 40\n                cur_st = (bx, by, chp, skmask, sgf, cjumps, sthrows, sbombs,\n                          sboxes, stnts, ccm, spit_m, sdestr_m, (), cturn)\n                esc_pair = self._find_fast_bomb_escape(bx, by, chp, cjumps, cturn, grid, hw, occ,\n                                                       has_live_lasers, Specifically, the heuristic's cost incorrectly increases when the hero moves towards a box that's further from a plate (e.g. `Box 4`). This inefficiency is why the solver seems to prefer to push the boxes away from their assigned plate, and led to the observed node count and runtime. I'm focusing on how to make the heuristic more responsive to changes in box positions.


**Uncovering Solver Inefficiency**

I've just had a breakthrough on the solver's inefficiency! The interleaving of box pushes is costing us dearly. Pushing `Box 1` then `Box 2`, compared to pushing all of `Box 1` first, results in an identical `mval`. However, the interleaved approach generates a massive search tree. My solution involves the straight-line push macro, which is the super-weapon, and should massively help the algorithm. My main focus is on the macro, and applying a proper `mval` weight to ensure it.


**Evaluating Heuristic Weights**

I'm now completely focused on fine-tuning the heuristic, specifically for `N2_4P5B` and understanding `NIGHTMARE_1_ICE_STOPPER`. After weighting the heuristic with `mval * 4 + len(unfilled) * 18`, completing a plate is worth `-22 * w` so the solver should immediately finish pushing each box to its plate, avoiding dithering. I am still investigating why `NIGHTMARE_1_ICE_STOPPER` got stuck and am now focusing on that scenario. The game pushed Box A onto a Spring, which led to a deadlock.


**Fixing Distance Calculations**

I've made a crucial discovery concerning `NIGHTMARE_1_ICE_STOPPER`. The primary issue was an incorrect distance calculation.  The heuristic was erroneously using hero BFS instead of the correct box reverse-pull BFS for calculating distances to plates.  This led to fundamentally flawed heuristic estimations, specifically miscalculating the distance from a box to a plate, which resulted in the solver getting trapped in local minimums. I need to implement the correct reverse-pull BFS to fix this inaccuracy and improve the solver's performance. The bug has been identified and I'm ready to fix it.


**Confirming Deadlock Resolution**

Now, I've got a solid fix for the deadlock in `NIGHTMARE_1_ICE_STOPPER`. After fixing the box distance calculation to use reverse-pull BFS for distance to plates, and incorporating the plate matching weight and early plate subgoals, the solver *instantly* recognizes deadlocks and produces the correct solution. I can see the algorithm working flawlessly, with the heuristic now correctly guiding the search. The corrected calculation ensures the distances are accurate, and that we have a functional solution.

px) + abs(by - py)\n                row.append(d)\n            costs.append(row)\n\n        # Bitmask DP over free_boxes (size <= 64)\n        dp_m = {0: 0}\n        for i in range(nu):\n            ndp = {}\n            crow = costs[i]\n            for mask, val in dp_m.items():\n                for bi in range(nb):\n                    bit = 1 << bi\n                    if not (mask & bit):\n                        nmask = mask | bit\n                        nv = val + crow[bi]\n                        prev = ndp.get(nmask)\n                        if prev is None or nv < prev:\n                            ndp[nmask] = nv\n            dp_m = ndp\n        best_val = 99999\n        best_mask = 0\n        for mask, val in dp_m.items():\n            if val < best_val:\n                best_val = val\n                best_mask = mask\n        return best_val, best_mask\n\n    def heuristic(self, st, plates_only=False):\n        (hx, hy, hp, kmask, gf, jumps, throws, bombs_left,\n         boxes, tnts, crack_m, pit_m, destr_m, active_bombs, turn) = st\n\n        h = 0\n        unfilled = [i for i, p in enumerate(self.plates_tuple) if p not in boxes]\n        if unfilled:\n            free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if b not in self.plates_set]\n            mval, mmask = self._min_plate_matching(unfilled, free_boxes)\n            if mval >= 99999:\n                return 99999\n            h += mval * 2 + len(unfilled) * 8\n            nearest = 999\n            for bi, (bx, by) in enumerate(free_boxes):\n                if mmask & (1 << bi):\n                    hd = abs(hx - My weighted A* with heuristics penalizes moves that seemingly increase the distance to the plate. This makes it avoid pushing the box UP, which is required. Hero at (8,21)\n    # Walk to (2,20) and push BOX_3(3,20) RIGHT onto SPRING_R(5,20) -> lands on PLATE_3(7,20)!\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 2)                # (8,23)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 6)                # (2,23)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 3)                # (2,20)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # push BOX_3 -> PLATE_3(7,20)! Hero at (4,20)\n    # Walk to (8,19) and push BOX_4(8,20) DOWN to PLATE_4(8,24)!\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 2)                # (4,18)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 4)                # (8,18)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 5)                # (8,19) then push BOX_4 -> PLATE_4(8,24)! Hero at (8,23)\n\n    assert eng.is_plates_active(), \"Plates should be active!\"\n\n    # --- 3. Go to K2 FIRST (since B_GATE is ALREADY OPEN while gf=False!       "content": "\"\"\"\nCompact, Tightened Championship v2.2 Demon Dungeon (\"Neon Sovereign Vault\").\nTarget: ~2,820G (< 3000G), author_solution ~175 steps (<= 200),\n100% v2.2 compliant, ZERO cheeses, crushes normal & strong FULL-mode solvers!\n\"\"\"\n\nimport time\nfrom sim_v22 import EngineV22, validate_dungeon, GRID_WIDTH, GRID_HEIGHT, TILE_COSTS\nfrom solver_v22 import FastWorld\n\n\ndef build_neon_dungeon():\n    grid = [\n        [\n            \"T_W\" if (x == 0 or x == GRID_WIDTH - 1 or y == 0 or y == GRID_HEIGHT - 1) else \"EMPTY\"\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)\n        ]\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT)\n    ]\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # ACT 1: TWO COUPLED SPRING-STOPPER SOKOBAN PUZZLES (4 PLATEs, 4 BOXes)\n    # Tightly placed near Spawn (x=2..8) to keep author_solution compact!\n    # Greedy-trap titanium pockets on FREE y=0 and y=25 borders!\n    # =========================================================================\n\n    # Upper Coupled Spring-Stopper (PLATE_1(6,5), stopper(7,5), PLATE_2 pocket(7,1)):\n    grid[4][6] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[6][6] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[5][8] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[5][4] = \"SPRING_R\"   # k=1:(5,5) PIT, k=2:(6,5) PLATE_1, k=3:(7,5) stopper\n    grid[5][5] = \"PIT\"\n    grid[5][6] = \"PLATE\"      # PLATE 1\n    grid[5][2] = \"BOX\"        # BOX 1\n    grid[3][7] = \"BOX\"        # BOX 2 (2 steps below greedy trap PLATE_2(7,1)!)\n    grid[1][6] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[1][7] = \"PLATE\"      # PLATE 2\n    grid[1][8] = \"T_W\"\n\n    # Lower Coupled Spring-Stopper (PLATE_3(6,20), stopper(7,20), PLATE_4 pocket(7,24)):\n    grid[19][6] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[21][6] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[20][8] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[20][4] = \"SPRING_R\"  # k=1:(5,20) PIT, k=2:(6,20) PLATE_3, k=3:(7,20) stopper\n    grid[20][5] = \"PIT\"\n    grid[20][6] = \"PLATE\"     # PLATE 3\n    grid[20][2] = \"BOX\"       # BOX 3\n    grid[22][7] = \"BOX\"       # BOX 4 (2 steps above greedy trap PLATE_4(7,24)!)\n    grid[24][6] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[24][7] = \"PLATE\"     # PLATE 4\n    grid[24][8] = \"T_W\"\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # ACT 2: HIDDEN BANK-SHOT THROW SWITCH VAULT (y=1..2, x=9..16)\n    # Triggered via THROW_U from (15,4) -> ONEWAY_D(15,2) -> MIRROR_NW(15,1)\n    # -> EMPTY(14,1) -> EMPTY(13,1) -> ICE(12,1) -> ICE(11,1) -> SWITCH(10,1)!\n    # Shares T_W(16,1) with K1 Vault!\n    # =========================================================================\n    grid[1][9] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[1][10] = \"SWITCH\"\n    grid[1][11] = \"ICE\"\n    grid[1][12] = \"ICE\"\n    grid[1][13] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[1][14] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[1][15] = \"MIRROR_NW\"\n    for x in range(10, 15):\n        grid[2][x] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[2][15] = \"ONEWAY_D\"\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # ACT 3: K1 VAULT (y=1..3, x=16..25) — P_GATE + R_GATE + Unavoidable\n    # SPRING_R -> MIMIC #1 (-45 HP) + Mandatory BOMB #1 (W) + ONEWAY_D Exit!\n    # =========================================================================\n    grid[1][16] = \"T_W\"       # shared with SWITCH vault!\n    grid[1][17] = \"SPRING_R\"  # launches k=3 to MIMIC(20,1)\n    grid[1][18] = \"PIT\"\n    grid[1][19] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[1][20] = \"MIMIC\"     # Unavoidable -45 HP!\n    grid[1][21] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[1][22] = \"EMPTY\"     # place BOMB #1 -> escape L to (21,1)->(20,1)\n    grid[1][23] = \"W\"         # Mandatory BOMB #1!\n    grid[1][24] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[1][25] = \"T_W\"\n\n    grid[2][16] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[2][17] = \"R_GATE\"    # Requires SWITCH ON (gf=True)!\n    for x in range(18, 24):\n        grid[2][x] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[2][24] = \"K1\"        # (24,2): x in [12,34], y in [2,10]\n    grid[2][25] = \"T_W\"\n\n    grid[3][17] = \"P_GATE\"\n    grid[3][24] = \"ONEWAY_D\"  # Exit ONLY!\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # ACT 4: K2 VAULT (y=22..24, x=16..25) — Exact Mirror with B_GATE!\n    # Unavoidable SPRING_R -> MIMIC #2 (-45 HP -> 10 HP!) + Mandatory BOMB #2!\n    # =========================================================================\n    grid[24][16] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[24][17] = \"SPRING_R\"\n    grid[24][18] = \"PIT\"\n    grid[24][19] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[24][20] = \"MIMIC\"    # Unavoidable -45 HP!\n    grid[24][21] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[24][22] = \"EMPTY\"    # place BOMB #2 -> escape L to (21,24)->(20,24)\n    grid[24][23] = \"W\"        # Mandatory BOMB #2!\n    grid[24][24] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[24][25] = \"T_W\"\n\n    grid[23][16] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[23][17] = \"B_GATE\"   # Requires SWITCH OFF (gf=False)!\n    for x in range(18, 24):\n        grid[23][x] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[23][24] = \"K2\"       # (24,23): |24-24|+|2-23|=21>=12!\n    grid[23][25] = \"T_W\"\n\n    grid[22][17] = \"P_GATE\"\n    grid[22][24] = \"ONEWAY_U\" # Exit ONLY!\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # ACT 5: THE 10-HP FINAL GAUNTLET TO V(35, 13)\n    # BOX_5(25,13) fills PIT(27,13) -> JUMP_R #1 over PIT(28,13) onto ONEWAY_R(29,13)\n    # -> P_GATE(30,13) -> 12-Beat Crossfire at (31,13) [LASER_D(31,7)+LASER2_U(31,19)]\n    # -> JUMP_R #2 over PIT(32,13) -> (33,13) -> JUMP_R #3 over PIT(34,13) -> V(35,13)!\n    # ALL 3 JUMPS STRICTLY REQUIRED!\n    # =========================================================================\n    grid[13][25] = \"BOX\"      # BOX 5 (2 pushes right to fill PIT(27,13)!)\n    for x in (29, 30, 32, 33, 34, 35):\n        grid[12][x] = \"T_W\"\n        grid[14][x] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[13][36] = \"T_W\"\n    grid[12][31] = \"ONEWAY_U\" # blocks entering from N, lets LASER_D shine!\n    grid[14][31] = \"ONEWAY_D\" # blocks entering from S, lets LASER2_U shine!\n\n    grid[13][27] = \"PIT\"      # filled by BOX 5\n    grid[13][28] = \"PIT\"      # JUMP_R #1\n    grid[13][29] = \"ONEWAY_R\" # point of no return!\n    grid[13][30] = \"P_GATE\"\n    grid[13][31] = \"EMPTY\"    # 12-beat crossfire at 10 HP!\n    grid[13][32] = \"PIT\"      # JUMP_R #2\n    grid[13][33] = \"EMPTY\"\n    grid[13][34] = \"PIT\"      # JUMP_R #3\n    grid[13][35] = \"V\"        # (35,13): x in [33,37], y in [4,21]!\n\n    grid[7][31] = \"LASER_D\"   # period 3 (hits y=8..17)\n    grid[19][31] = \"LASER2_U\" # period 4 (hits y=9..18)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # ACT 6: MID-FIELD 12-BEAT ICE GUILLOTINE (x=11..14, y=13)\n    # Straight East from Spawn (2,13): Turn 1..8 walk to (10,13), Turn 9 slide\n    # across ICE(11..13,13) onto (14,13) -> hit on Turn 9 (LASER_D) / Turn 12 INSTA-KILL!\n    # =========================================================================\n    grid[13][11] = \"ICE\"\n    grid[13][12] = \"ICE\"\n    grid[13][13] = \"ICE\"\n    grid[6][14] = \"LASER_D\"   # period 3 (hits y=7..16)\n    grid[20][14] = \"LASER2_U\" # period 4 (hits y=10..19)\n\n    return grid\n\n\ndef build_author_solution_exact(grid):\n    eng = EngineV22(grid)\n    acts = []\n\n    def do(seq):\n        for a in seq:\n            acts.append(a)\n            st = eng.step(a)\n            if st == \"DEAD\":\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Author died at step {len(acts)} ({a}) pos=({eng.hx},{eng.hy}) hp={eng.hp}\")\n            if st == \"CLEARED\":\n                return True\n        return False\n\n    # 1. Upper Coupled Spring-Stopper (from (2,13))\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 11)               # (2,2)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 5)                # (7,2)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 2)                # push BOX_2 (7,3)->(7,5), hero (7,4)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 2)                # (7,2)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 6)                # (1,2)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 3)                # (1,5)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # push BOX_1 (2,5)->SPRING_R(4,5)->PLATE_1(6,5), hero (3,5)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 2)                # (3,7)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 4)                # (7,7)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 5)                # push BOX_2 (7,5)->PLATE_2(7,1), hero (7,2)\n\n    # 2. Lower Coupled Spring-Stopper (go down x=9 to (9,23) -> (7,23))\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # (9,2)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 21)               # (9,23)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 2)                # (7,23)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 2)                # push BOX_4 (7,22)->(7,20), hero (7,21)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 2)                # (7,23)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 6)                # (1,23)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 3)                # (1,20)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # push BOX_3 (2,20)->SPRING_R(4,20)->PLATE_3(6,20), hero (3,20)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 2)                # (3,18)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 4)                # (7,18)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 5)                # push BOX_4 (7,20)->PLATE_4(7,24), hero (7,23)\n    assert eng.is_plates_active(), \"Plates not active!\"\n\n    # 3. K2 Vault FIRST (gf=False -> B_GATE(17,23) is OPEN!)\n    # From (7,23) -> (17,21) via y=21 (below LASER2_U(14,20) which fires UP -> 100% laser-free!)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 2)                # (7,21)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 10)               # (17,21)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 3)                # (17,22 P_GATE)->(17,23 B_GATE)->(17,24 SPRING_R)->MIMIC(20,24) [-45 HP, hp=55]!\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # (22,24)\n    do([\"BOMB\"])                      # BOMB #1 at (22,24)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 2)                # (21,24)->(20,24) [destroys W(23,24)!]\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 4)                # (24,24)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 3)                # (24,23 K2)->(24,22 ONEWAY_U)->(24,21)\n    assert eng.keys == [False, True], f\"K2 not picked! {eng.keys}\"\n\n    # 4. Bank-Shot THROW_U at (15,4) -> flips SWITCH(10,1) (gf=True -> R_GATE(17,2) OPENS!)\n    # From (24,21) -> (15,4) staying on x=15..24 (100% clear of x=14 and x=31 lasers!)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 9)                # (15,21)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 17)               # (15,4)\n    do([\"THROW_U\"])                   # bank-shot through ONEWAY_D(15,2)+MIRROR_NW(15,1)+ICE -> SWITCH(10,1)!\n    assert eng.gates_flipped is True, \"Switch did not flip!\"\n\n    # 5. K1 Vault (gf=True -> R_GATE(17,2) is OPEN!)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # (17,4)\n    do([\"MOVE_U\"] * 3)                # (17,3 P_GATE)->(17,2 R_GATE)->(17,1 SPRING_R)->MIMIC(20,1) [-45 HP, hp=10]!\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # (22,1)\n    do([\"BOMB\"])                      # BOMB #2 at (22,1)\n    do([\"MOVE_L\"] * 2)                # (21,1)->(20,1) [destroys W(23,1)!]\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 4)                # (24,1)\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 3)                # (24,2 K1)->(24,3 ONEWAY_D)->(24,4)\n    assert eng.keys == [True, True] and eng.hp == 10, f\"K1 check failed: keys={eng.keys} hp={eng.hp}\"\n\n    # 6. Final 10-HP Gauntlet to V(35,13)!\n    do([\"MOVE_D\"] * 9)                # (24,13)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"] * 2)                # push BOX_5 (25,13)->(27,13 PIT filled!), hero (26,13)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"])                    # (27,13)\n    do([\"JUMP_R\"])                    # JUMP #1 over PIT(28,13) -> ONEWAY_R(29,13)\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"])                    # (30,13) P_GATE\n    while (eng.turn % 12) not in (1, 10):\n        do([\"WAIT\"])\n    do([\"MOVE_R\"])                    # (31,13) safely on turn%12 in {1,10}\n    do([\"JUMP_R\"])                    # JUMP #2 over PIT(32,13) -> (33,13) safely on turn%12 in {2,11}\n    cleared = do([\"JUMP_R\"])          # JUMP #3 over PIT(34,13) -> V(35,13) CLEARED!\n    assert cleared and eng.status == \"CLEARED\"\n    return acts, eng\n\n\ndef main():\n    grid = build_neon_dungeon()\n    counts = {}\n    spent = 0\n    for y in range(1, 25):\n        for x in range(1, 39):\n            t = grid[y][x]\n            counts[t] = counts.get(t, 0) + 1\n            spent += TILE_COSTS[t]\n    print(f\"Spent Gold: {spent}G / 3000G (Vault: {3000 - spent}G)\")\n    print(\"Tile counts:\", {k: v for k, v in sorted(counts.items()) if k != \"EMPTY\"})\n\n    sol, eng = build_author_solution_exact(grid)\n    print(f\"\\nAuthor solution VERIFIED: {len(sol)} steps | hp={eng.hp} | jumps={eng.jumps} | throws={eng.throws} | bombs={eng.bombs_left}\")\n\n    ok, errs, info = validate_dungeon({\n        \"taunt\": \"双挡板锁金门，残血斩双频！\",\n        \"grid\": grid,\n        \"author_solution\": sol,\n    })\n    print(f\"validate_dungeon: ok={ok} | errs={errs} | safe_t1={len(info.get('safe_t1_coords', []))}\")\n\n    # RED-TEAM: Test our OWN 21/21 Championship Solver (2.0s budget AND 6.0s budget!)\n    for bud in (2.0, 6.0):\n        e_rt = EngineV22(grid)\n        fw = FastWorld()\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\n        st0 = fw.build_from_state(e_rt.get_observation())\n        plan, partial, stats = fw.solve(st0, budget_sec=bud)\n        dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000\n        print(f\"\\nRED-TEAM (Our 21/21 Solver, {bud:.1f}s): solved={plan is not None} | nodes={stats['nodes']} | time={dt:.1f}ms\")\n        if plan is not None:\n            e2 = EngineV22(grid)\n            for a in plan:\n                e2.step(a)\n                if e2.status != \"RUNNING\":\n                    break\n            print(f\"  CRACKED! len={len(plan)} hp={e2.hp} jumps={e2.jumps} throws={e2.throws} bombs={e2.bombs_left}\")\n        else:\n            e2 = EngineV22(grid)\n            for a in partial:\n                e2.step(a)\n            boxes = [(x, y) for y in range(26) for x in range(40) if e2.occupant[y][x] == \"BOX\"]\n            print(f\"  SURVIVED! partial len={len(partial)} end=({e2.hx},{e2.hy}) hp={e2.hp} keys={e2.keys} pact={e2.is_plates_active()} boxes={boxes}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },

      {
        "type": "text",
        "text": "Wrote file successfully: design_dungeon.py"

**Analyzing Impressive Output**

I am absolutely blown away by the output! The author's solution is verified with an incredibly low step count, and the resource expenditure is minimal. The validation results are also positive, showing no errors. This feels like a significant breakthrough!


**Refining Deadlock Detection**

I'm now zeroing in on a critical issue: irreversible-pocket deadlocks. Even my strongest solver is getting snagged. The core insight is that an intelligent opponent might leverage these traps. My immediate goal is to enhance my solver with approximately ten lines of code to identify and circumvent these deadlock scenarios, even in my own designed dungeons.


**Formalizing Deadlock Conditions**

I've distilled a crucial deadlock rule based on initial conditions and box placement. Specifically, I'm defining 'stopper-required plates' that can only be reached with a spring/ice trajectory and 'pocket plates' that trap a box permanently. My rule states that if `k` stopper-required plates remain unfilled, we require at least `k` mobile boxes available to act as stoppers. This rule seems 100% mathematically sound.


**Testing New Heuristic**

I'm now implementing a new heuristic penalty within `heuristic()` to discourage premature pocket-plate filling when stopper-required plates are unfilled. Specifically, I'm adding a penalty of +40 for each pocket plate filled before addressing stopper plates. The key is that this shouldn't prune correct solutions. I'm excited to see if this enhances the solver's performance.



**Optimizing Demon Dungeon Design**

I'm now focused on pushing the Demon Dungeon to its absolute limit! I'm targeting a higher score by forcing `DEAD` outcomes for failing solvers instead of `TIMEOUT`. Analyzing red-team logs reveals that my solver's ability to model `mod 3`, `mod 4`, and `exposed` start-cell conditions allows it to survive at the `SWITCH` throw spot (`15,4`) with 100 HP, resulting in `TIMEOUT` (~490 pts), instead of the `DEAD` scenario (850+ pts) I'm aiming for.


**Designing Deceptive Trap**

I've got a killer idea for a trap! The goal is to force rivals to die instantly. The plan is to intersect lasers right on the `THROW_U` spot, ensuring instant death for any solver that falls for it. I'm focusing on aligning lasers to exploit a weakness in the rival's `mod 4` handling.


**Implementing Laser Overhaul**

I've just finalized a devastating dual-laser trap strategy designed for instant death! This utilizes lasers at `(15, 14)` and `(23, 4)` to inflict damage at `(15, 4)` on the throw. Executing `THROW_U` on a `turn % 12 == 0` is instant death, and even with clever play, certain turns lead to a fatal outcome. The author's solution, however, easily bypasses this deadly crossfire, highlighting the trap's sophistication. This upgrade is a major win!


**Confirming Key Vulnerability**

I've made a crucial discovery! A single laser at (23, 4) simultaneously guards the `THROW_U` spot (15, 4) and the entrance to the `K1` vault (17, 4)!  This significantly narrows the possible solutions. Furthermore, another laser at (15, 14) protects both the `THROW_U` spot and the mid-field ice guillotine. I'm checking the author's solution steps. The author needs to time this step exactly, in order to not be killed by the lasers.


**Refining Deadly Trajectory**

I've been working on a cunning deadly laser strategy! I realized there's no way to escape the laser if I *begin* the `THROW_U` from `(15, 4)`, because the turn sequence will always result in a multiple of 3. However, if I start at `(15, 3)`, I'll still be killed!


**Verifying Safety Strategy**

I've just had a major breakthrough on the laser pathing. It seems if I approach (15, 3) through (15, 4), I can actually avoid damage using a 12-beat pattern, specifically when t % 12 is 1 or 10. The laser at (15, 14) STOPS at (15, 4), and so (15, 3) is safe! This could be a new solution!
